### [1] [Cross-level Privacy Preserving Utility Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00036)
*Jiahong Cai,Wensheng Gan,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出跨层级隐私保护效用挖掘（CLPPUM）问题，针对带有分类信息的数据集，设计了三种基于不同受害者项选择策略的算法（Min-RF、Max-RF、Best-NSCF）以及GI-dic字典结构，实验证明该系列算法能有效隐藏所有敏感跨层级高效用项集且不引入人工项集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护效用挖掘（PPUM）方法未考虑数据集中的分类层次信息，导致处理泛化项时存在困难，亟需一种能处理跨层级敏感高效用项集的隐私保护方法。

Method: 提出CLPPUM问题定义；基于三种受害者项选择策略（最小RGISU优先、最大RGISU优先、最佳NSC优先）开发Min-RF、Max-RF和Best-NSCF三种算法；设计名为GI-dic的新型字典结构加速效用指标计算。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，所提算法能成功隐藏所有敏感跨层级高效用项集且不引入人工项集；在稀疏数据集上表现良好；Min-RF和Best-NSCF一致优于Max-RF；Min-RF在低最小效用阈值和稠密数据集上性能最佳。

Conclusion: 跨层级隐私保护效用挖掘问题可被有效解决，其中Min-RF算法综合表现最优，为带层次信息的数据隐私保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving utility mining (PPUM) aims to hide sensitive high-utility patterns while preserving the utility of the sanitized database. In practice, however, many datasets are associated with taxonomic information, which makes the identification and processing of generalized items more challenging. To address this, we investigate the cross-level privacy-preserving utility mining (CLPPUM) problem and propose a method for protecting generalized items. Based on different victim item selection strategies, we develop three CLPPUM algorithms: minimum RGISU first (Min-RF), maximum RGISU first (Max-RF), and best NSC first (Best-NSCF). Furthermore, to enable efficient victim item identification, a novel dictionary structure named GI-dic is designed to accelerate the computation of required utility metrics. Experimental results on multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed algorithms successfully hide all sensitive cross-level high-utility itemsets without introducing artificial itemsets. The results also show that our method performs well on sparse datasets, and both Min-RF and Best-NSCF consistently outperform Max-RF. Overall, Min-RF achieves the best performance, particularly when the minimum utility threshold is low and the dataset is dense.

</details>


### [2] [SiriusHelper: An LLM Agent-Based Operations Assistant for Big Data Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00043)
*Yu Shen,Shiyang Liu,Qihang He,Yihang Cheng,Haining Xie,Zhiming He,Huahua Fan,Xianzhi Tan,Teng Ma,Shaoquan Zhang,Danqing Huang,Fan Jiang,Yang Li,Chongqing Zhao,Peng Chen,Jie Jiang,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SiriusHelper是一个部署于腾讯大数据平台的智能助手，通过统一在线服务、DeepSearch驱动的多跳检索、分层知识库以及自动化工单分析与SOP蒸馏机制，在通用咨询和专业故障排查场景中提升回答准确性与效率，并将在线工单量减少20.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM+RAG助手在大数据平台实际部署中面临三大挑战：场景覆盖有限（通用咨询与专业诊断难以兼顾）、知识访问低效（缺乏多跳检索且知识组织扁平化）、维护成本高（工单非结构化，难以转化为助手改进和可复用SOP）。

Method: SiriusHelper包含三个核心机制：1) 统一在线助手，自动识别用户意图并将查询路由至合适处理路径（含专家工作流，如SQL执行诊断）；2) DeepSearch驱动的优先级分层知识库，实现多跳检索且避免上下文过载；3) 自动化工单理解与SOP蒸馏，诊断助手失败原因（缺失知识或错误路由）并提取领域SOP以持续丰富知识库。

Result: 在腾讯大数据平台的实验与在线部署中，SiriusHelper优于代表性基线方法，并将在线工单量降低20.8%。

Conclusion: SiriusHelper通过意图路由、多跳检索和自动化工单分析三管齐下，有效解决了大数据平台智能助手在场景覆盖、知识检索和维护成本方面的实际问题，验证了其在工业部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Big data platforms are widely used in modern enterprises, and an in-production intelligent assistant is increasingly important to help users quickly find actionable guidance and reduce operational burden. While recent LLM+RAG assistants provide a natural interface, they face practical challenges in real deployments: limited scenario coverage across both general consultation and domain-specific troubleshooting workflows, inefficient knowledge access due to inadequate multi-hop retrieval and flat knowledge organization, and high maintenance cost because escalated tickets are unstructured and hard to convert into assistant improvements and reusable SOPs.
  In this paper, we present SiriusHelper, a deployed intelligent assistant for big data platforms. SiriusHelper serves as a unified online assistant that automatically identifies user intent and routes queries to the right handling path, including dedicated expert workflows for specialized scenarios (e.g., SQL execution diagnosis). To support complex troubleshooting, SiriusHelper combines a DeepSearch-driven mechanism with a priority-based hierarchical knowledge base to enable multi-hop retrieval without context overload, thus improving answer reliability and latency. To reduce expert overhead, SiriusHelper further introduces automated ticket understanding and SOP distillation: it diagnoses the assistant failure reason (e.g., missing knowledge or wrong routing) and extracts domain-specific SOPs to continuously enrich the knowledge base. Experiments and online deployment on Tencent Big Data platform show that SiriusHelper outperforms representative alternatives and reduces online ticket volume by 20.8\%.

</details>


### [3] [EGREFINE: An Execution-Grounded Optimization Framework for Text-to-SQL Schema Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00628)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yu Pang,Rui Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EGRefine 是一个四阶段流水线，通过将模式优化建模为约束优化问题，利用非破坏性 SQL 视图重命名模糊列，从而提升 Text-to-SQL 的执行准确率，并保证查询等价性和列级别无退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据库模式常存在命名歧义、缩写或不一致，导致 Text-to-SQL 模型准确率下降。现有方法将模式视为固定输入，仅在后续环节纠正错误，缺乏从源头优化模式命名以提升下游任务的系统方法。

Method: 将模式精炼形式化为一个约束优化问题——寻找最大化下游 Text-to-SQL 执行准确率的列重命名函数，同时通过数据库视图保证查询等价性。由于该问题计算困难，采用列级贪心分解策略，提出 EGRefine 四阶段流水线：(1) 筛选歧义列，(2) 生成上下文感知的候选名称，(3) 通过执行反馈进行验证（含保守选择规则保证列级非退化），(4) 将结果物化为非破坏性 SQL 视图（保证数据库级查询等价性）。

Result: 在控制性模式退化基准、真实世界基准和企业级基准上，EGRefine 能有效恢复因模式命名噪声而损失的准确率，并在任务超出当前 Text-to-SQL 能力时正确放弃。精炼后的模式可跨模型家族迁移，实现一次精炼、多模型部署。

Conclusion: EGRefine 通过视图机制实现了安全的模式重命名，具备列级非退化和查询等价性两个结构性保证，是一种有效且可迁移的 Text-to-SQL 模式优化方法。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL enables non-expert users to query databases in natural language, yet real-world schemas often suffer from ambiguous, abbreviated, or inconsistent naming conventions that degrade model accuracy. Existing approaches treat schemas as fixed and address errors downstream. In this paper, we frame schema refinement as a constrained optimization problem: find a renaming function that maximizes downstream Text-to-SQL execution accuracy while preserving query equivalence through database views. We analyze the computational hardness of this problem, which motivates a column-wise greedy decomposition, and instantiate it as EGRefine: a four-phase pipeline that screens ambiguous columns, generates context-aware candidate names, verifies them through execution-grounded feedback, and materializes the result as non-destructive SQL views. The pipeline carries two structural properties: column-local non-degradation, ensured by the conservative selection rule in the verification phase, and database-level query equivalence, ensured by the view-based materialization phase. Together they make the resulting refinement safe by construction at the column level, with cross-column and prompt-level interactions handled empirically rather than analytically. Across controlled schema-degradation, real-world, and enterprise benchmarks, EGRefine recovers accuracy lost to schema naming noise where applicable and correctly abstains where the underlying task exceeds current Text-to-SQL capabilities, with refined schemas transferring across model families to enable refine-once, serve-many-models deployment. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/EGRefine.

</details>


### [4] [Living Databases: A Unified Model for Continuous Schema Evolution, Versioning, and Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00676)
*Amol Deshpande*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出统一数据库演化功能（更新、版本控制、模式变更、流操作等）的单一抽象框架，集成溯源追踪、条件传播、可配置变更告警，并基于Prolly Tree实现原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库和数据集通过更新、转换、版本控制、模式变更、流操作等多种机制持续演化，但这些功能传统上被孤立研究，各自拥有独立的抽象、算法和系统实现，缺乏统一的处理框架。

Method: 提出一种统一的抽象和计算原语集合，集成系统可见操作的溯源追踪、更新的条件传播、变更事件的可配置告警；通过声明式机制控制视图和机器学习模型等派生对象的演化；基于类Merkle树的数据结构"Prolly Tree"进行原型实现。

Result: 该框架能够覆盖现有的各种使用场景并支持新的应用场景，在关系型数据库系统中基于Prolly Tree实现了原型并取得初步实验成果。

Conclusion: 数据库的多种演化功能可以被统一在单一抽象和共同的计算原语之下，提供比之前方法更全面的集成能力，为依赖对象（如视图、ML模型）的演化提供声明式控制机制。

Abstract: Databases, and datasets more generally, evolve continuously through updates, transformations, versioning, schema changes, streaming operations, and other mechanisms. While prior work has noted connections among some of these areas, they have traditionally been studied in isolation, each with its own abstractions, algorithms, and system implementations. In this paper, we argue for unifying these diverse functionalities under a single abstraction and a common set of computational primitives. We present such an abstraction, powerful enough to encompass existing use cases and to support new ones. Going beyond previous approaches, our framework seamlessly integrates provenance tracking for system-visible operations, conditional propagation of updates, and configurable alerts on change events. It also offers a principled treatment of dependent objects such as views and derived artifacts like machine learning models, by providing declarative mechanisms to control their evolution. Finally, we sketch a prototype implementation in a relational-like database system based on an adaptation of the "Prolly Tree", a Merkle tree-inspired data structure with tunable parameters to meet varying performance requirements, and present some initial experimental results.

</details>


### [5] [Complete Integration of Team Project-based Learning into a Database Syllabus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00736)
*S. Iserte,V. R. Tomas,M. Pérez,M. Castillo,P. Boronat,L. A. García*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在Jaume I大学计算机科学专业高级数据库课程中实施的团队项目式学习（TPBL）方法，详细描述了项目设计（团队组建、活动、时间表、学习能力）以及从三个维度（队友互评、学生意见、最终成绩）对四年（2018/19-2021/2022）教学效果的评估，结果显示该方法取得积极成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将项目式学习（PBL）与团队协作相结合，使学生既能通过真实项目开发获得实践经验，又能锻炼团队合作能力，从而更贴近真实工作场景，提升学习效果。

Method: 在Jaume I大学计算机科学专业高级数据库课程中，连续四年（2018/19-2021/2022）实施TPBL教学，包含团队组建、项目活动设计、时间表规划及软技能与专业技能训练，并从队友互评、学生主观意见和课程最终成绩三个维度进行评估。

Result: 四年的教学实践取得了积极成果，学生对TPBL方法反馈良好，项目成果和课程成绩均表现正向。

Conclusion: TPBL在高级数据库课程中是有效的教学方法，能够融合PBL和团队协作的优势，值得在相关课程中推广。

Abstract: Team project-based learning (TPBL) combines two learning techniques: project-based learning (PBL) and teamwork. This combination leverages the learning outcomes of both methods and places students in a real work situation where they must develop and solve a real project while working as a team. TPBL has been used in two advanced database subjects in Jaume I University (UJI)'s Computer Science degree program. This learning method was used for four years (academic years from 2018/19 to 2021/2022) with positive outcomes. This study presents the project development, which includes teamwork formation, activities, timetable, and exercised learning competencies (both soft and specific). Further, the project's results were evaluated from three different perspectives: a) teamwork evaluation by teammates, b) Students' opinions on the subject and project, and c) subject final grades.

</details>


### [6] [Multiset semantics in SPARQL, Relational Algebra and Datalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00417)
*Renzo Angles,Claudio Gutierrez,Daniel Hernández*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了SPARQL模式在多集合语义下的代数与逻辑结构，通过将SPARQL与Datalog和关系代数对齐，证明了非递归Datalog（带安全否定与多集合支持）和多集合关系代数在表达能力上与SPARQL等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为SPARQL的多集合语义建立严格的代数与逻辑基础，并将其与成熟的查询语言（Datalog和关系代数）进行形式化对齐。

Method: 将SPARQL（AND, UNION, FILTER, EXCEPT, SELECT）分别映射到两种形式化语言：（i）支持多集合的非递归Datalog（带安全否定），（ii）包含投影、选择、自然连接、算术并、except操作的多集合关系代数，并证明三者表达等价性。

Result: 证明了SPARQL、扩展的非递归Datalog、多集合关系代数三者之间在表达能力强弱上是等价的。

Conclusion: SPARQL的多集合语义可被嵌入到经典查询语言框架中，为SPARQL的优化和形式化推理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The paper analyzes and characterizes the algebraic and logical structure of the multiset semantics for SPARQL patterns involving AND, UNION, FILTER, EXCEPT, and SELECT. To do this, we align SPARQL with two well-established query languages: Datalog and Relational Algebra. Specifically, we study (i) a version of non-recursive Datalog with safe negation extended to support multisets, and (ii) a multiset relational algebra comprising projection, selection, natural join, arithmetic union, and except. We prove that these three formalisms are expressively equivalent under multiset semantics.

</details>

### [7] [UniBCI: Towards a Unified Pretrained Model for Invasive Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00061)
*Binjie Hong,Rui Xiong,Liyuan Han,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出UniBCI，一个面向侵入式脑机接口的统一预训练模型，通过上下文条件时空标记化（CST）、层级区间-区域注意力（IAA）机制和可扩展的自监督掩码信号重构目标，在跨物种、跨主体、跨脑区和跨实验范式的大规模数据上预训练，在下游任务中实现了SOTA性能，并取得了准确性与效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有侵入式神经尖峰数据建模面临三大挑战：有限规模的非均匀数据、跨域分布偏移以及侵入式神经信号固有的时空复杂性，亟需一种统一的、可泛化的预训练模型来应对这些问题。

Method: UniBCI包含三个核心组件：(1) 上下文条件时空标记化（CST）方案，将神经信号与元数据嵌入共享表示空间；(2) 层级区间-区域注意力（IAA）机制，通过线性注意力捕获尖峰动态模式，通过滑动窗口注意力捕获局部依赖关系；(3) 可扩展的自监督掩码信号重构目标，从大规模无标签数据中学习可泛化的神经表示。此外，还提出了统一归一化和标记化方法以标准化异构记录。

Result: 在多种下游任务上达到SOTA性能，泛化能力提升，同时以更少的可训练参数和更低的推理延迟实现了准确性与效率的强平衡。

Conclusion: UniBCI为构建通用神经基础模型提供了实用的一步，实现了对侵入式神经数据的鲁棒、可扩展且可迁移的表示学习。

Abstract: Modeling invasive neural spike data is fundamental to advancing high-performance brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). However, existing approaches face critical challenges, including limited-scale heterogeneous data, cross-domain distribution shift, and the intrinsic spatiotemporal complexity of invasive neural signals. In this work, we propose UniBCI, a unified pretrained model for invasive Brain-Computer Interfaces. The model integrates three key components: (1) a context-conditioned spatio-temporal tokenization (CST) scheme that embeds neural signals together with metadata into a shared representation space; (2) a hierarchical Interval-Area Attention (IAA) mechanism that captures patterns of spike dynamics in slots via linear attention and locality dependencies via sliding-window attention; and (3) a scalable self-supervised masked signals reconstruction objective for learning generalizable neural representations from large-scale unlabeled data. We construct a pretraining corpus spanning multiple species, subjects, brain regions, and behavioral experiment paradigms. These heterogeneous recordings are standardize via our proposed unified normalization and tokenization. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that UniBCI achieves SOTA performance across diverse downstream tasks while improving generalization. Moreover, the model achieves a strong balance between accuracy and efficiency, with fewer trainable parameters and lower inference latency. These results suggest that UniBCI provides a practical step toward general-purpose neural foundation models, enabling robust, scalable, and transferable representation learning for invasive neural data. The code for this paper is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UniBCI-C805.

</details>


### [8] [Spiking Sequence Machines and Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00662)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文证明脉冲稀疏分布式记忆序列机（2007）与Transformer（2017）虽架构不同，但共享相同五步功能操作（编码、上下文维护、联想检索、存储、解码）和余弦相似度检索原语；提出相位-延迟同构定理证明正弦位置相位与脉冲时序线性相关，并证明点积注意力在该映射下保持位置分量全局尺度不变；实验表明位置表征的关键属性是点积相似度下的距离可区分性，而非正弦函数形式，时间、相位、秩是同一计算原语（有序索引）的三种实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不同序列学习架构（脉冲Sparse Distributed Memory与Transformer）在功能层面的统一性，识别序列学习底层共享的计算原语。

Method: 理论分析证明两者的五步功能同构性，提出相位-延迟同构定理并证明注意力不变性引理；通过频率压缩位置编码与学习型秩编码的实验对比验证核心假设。

Result: 频率压缩位置编码在位置敏感复制任务上无法收敛，学习型秩编码匹配或超越正弦编码性能。

Conclusion: 序列学习的本质是基于相似度的有序索引检索；位置表征的关键属性是点积相似度下的距离可区分性；时间、相位、秩是同一计算原语的不同实现。

Abstract: Sequence learning reduces to similarity-based retrieval over a temporally indexed representation space, a constraint on any sequence model, not a property of a specific architecture. We show that a spiking Sparse Distributed Memory sequence machine (2007) and the transformer (2017) independently instantiate the same five functional operations (encoding, context maintenance, associative retrieval, storage, and decoding), with cosine similarity as the shared retrieval primitive in both. We formalise a Phase-Latency Isomorphism showing that sinusoidal positional phase and spike timing are linearly related, and prove that dot product attention is invariant to this mapping up to a global scale factor on the positional component (Lemma 1). Empirically, frequency-compressed positional encoding fails to converge on a positionally demanding copy task, while a learned rank-based embedding matches or exceeds sinusoidal encoding, indicating that the critical property for positional representation is distance discriminability under dot-product similarity, not sinusoidal form. Time, phase, and rank are three instantiations of the same computational primitive, an ordered index whose structure survives similarity-based retrieval.

</details>

### [9] [Dynamic-TD3: A Novel Algorithm for UAV Path Planning with Dynamic Obstacle Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00059)
*Wentao Chen,Jingtang Chen,Mingjian Fu,Tiantian Li,Youfeng Su,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic-TD3框架，通过将导航建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），结合自适应轨迹关系演化机制（ATREM）和物理感知门控卡尔曼滤波（PAG-KF），在严格安全约束下实现无人机自主导航，兼顾机动性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在复杂高风险环境中无人机自主导航应用广泛，但面临安全-探索困境：软惩罚机制鼓励危险试错，而现有基于约束的方法在传感器噪声和意图不确定性下性能退化。

Method: 1) 将导航建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）；2) 引入自适应轨迹关系演化机制（ATREM）捕捉长程意图；3) 采用物理感知门控卡尔曼滤波（PAG-KF）减轻非平稳观测噪声；4) 通过拉格朗日松弛构建双准则策略，平衡任务效率与硬安全约束。

Result: 在激活动态威胁场景下，该方法展现优越的避碰性能、更低的能耗和更平滑的飞行轨迹。

Conclusion: Dynamic-TD3框架通过物理增强机制有效解决了安全-探索困境，在保证严格安全约束的同时维护了无人机机动性，为复杂高风险环境下的安全自主导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) finds extensive application in autonomous drone navigation within complex, high-risk environments. However, its practical deployment faces a safety-exploration dilemma: soft penalty mechanisms encourage risky trial-and-error, while most constraint-based methods suffer degraded performance under sensor noise and intent uncertainty. We propose Dynamic-TD3, a physically enhanced framework that enforces strict safety constraints while maintaining maneuverability by modeling navigation as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP). This framework integrates an Adaptive Trajectory Relational Evolution Mechanism (ATREM) to capture long-range intentions and employs a Physically Aware Gated Kalman Filter (PAG-KF) to mitigate non-stationary observation noise. The resulting state representation drives a dual-criterion policy that balances mission efficiency against hard safety constraints via Lagrangian relaxation. In experiments with aggressive dynamic threats, this approach demonstrates superior collision avoidance performance, reduced energy consumption, and smoother flight trajectories.

</details>


### [10] [Do Open-Loop Metrics Predict Closed-Loop Driving? A Cross-Benchmark Correlation Study of NAVSIM and Bench2Drive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00066)
*Yiru Wang,Anqing Jiang,Shuo Wang,Yuwen Heng,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了15种SOTA自动驾驶方法在NAVSIM（开环）和Bench2Drive（闭环）上的表现，发现NAVSIM v2的安全感知开环指标与闭环驾驶分数存在强正相关但非单调的关系，其中Ego Progress是最强单一预测因子，而安全指标NC预测力较弱。作者提出了一个简化的3指标公式（匹配5指标PDMS的预测能力），并指出开环中的安全-进度权衡在闭环中表现不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统开环指标（ADE/FDE）与闭环驾驶分数之间缺乏可靠相关性，本文探究NAVSIM v2新引入的安全感知开环指标能否弥合这一差距，从而验证开环评估对闭环性能的预测有效性。

Method: 系统交叉引用15种SOTA方法在NAVSIM（开环）和Bench2Drive（闭环）上的已发表结果，构建配对数据集（最终8种方法有完整配对数据），通过Spearman相关性分析、排名比较等方法评估各开环子指标对闭环驾驶分数的预测能力。

Result: (1) PDM Score与Driving Score强正相关但非单调，存在排名反转；(2) Ego Progress是最强单一预测因子，远超碰撞指标NC；(3) 开环中最大化安全牺牲进度的方法在闭环中因超时和低速惩罚而表现不佳；简化3指标公式（不含TTC和Comfort）与完整5指标PDMS达到相同Spearman ρ=0.90。

Conclusion: NAVSIM v2的安全感知开环指标部分弥合了与闭环性能的差距，但存在排名反转和开环-闭环安全-进度权衡不一致的问题；雪球效应（小开环偏差累积成闭环失败）是残余差距的候选机制；TTC和Comfort在当前SOTA方法中信息边际贡献有限。

Abstract: Open-loop evaluation offers fast, reproducible assessment of autonomous driving planners, but its ability to predict real closed-loop driving performance remains questionable. Prior work has shown that traditional open-loop metrics such as Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) exhibit no reliable correlation with closed-loop Driving Score. In this paper, we ask whether the more recent, safety-aware open-loop metrics introduced by NAVSIM~v2 can bridge this gap. By systematically cross-referencing published results from 15 state-of-the-art methods across NAVSIM (open-loop) and Bench2Drive (closed-loop), we compile a paired dataset of open-loop sub-metrics and closed-loop performance, yielding 8 methods with complete paired data. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (1) the aggregate NAVSIM PDM Score shows a strong positive but non-monotonic correlation with Bench2Drive Driving Score, with clear ranking inversions; (2) among individual NAVSIM sub-metrics, Ego Progress (EP) is the strongest single predictor of closed-loop success, substantially exceeding the safety-critical collision metric NC; (3) the safety-progress trade-off manifests differently in open-loop and closed-loop: methods that maximize safety at the expense of progress rank highly in NAVSIM but underperform in closed-loop due to timeout and slow-driving penalties. We further demonstrate that a much simpler 3-metric formula matches the predictive power of the full 5-metric PDMS at the same Spearman $ρ{=}0.90$ on our paired sample of $n{=}8$ methods, suggesting that within current state-of-the-art methods -- where TTC and Comfort approach saturation -- these two sub-metrics add little marginal information for closed-loop ranking. Additionally, we identify the snowball effect -- where small open-loop deviations compound into closed-loop failures -- as a candidate mechanism for the residual gap.

</details>


### [11] [Being-H0.7: A Latent World-Action Model from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00078)
*Hao Luo,Wanpeng Zhang,Yicheng Feng,Sipeng Zheng,Haiweng Xu,Chaoyi Xu,Ziheng Xi,Yuhui Fu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Being-H0.7 是一种潜空间世界-动作模型，通过在感知与动作之间插入可学习的潜变量查询，并采用未来感知的双分支训练设计（先验分支与后验分支对齐），在不生成未来视频帧的情况下，使VLA策略具备未来推理能力，在多个仿真与真实任务上达到SOTA或相当性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）存在动作监督稀疏的问题，容易学习捷径映射而非真正的动力学、接触和任务进度表征；而世界-动作模型的像素空间预测对控制而言成本高昂且间接，可能建模与动作生成无关的视觉细节且引入大量训练/推理开销。

Method: 提出Being-H0.7，在VLA的感知与动作之间插入可学习的潜变量查询作为紧凑推理接口；采用未来感知双分支训练：可部署的先验分支从当前上下文推断潜状态，仅训练使用的后验分支用未来观测嵌入替换查询；在潜推理空间对齐两个分支，使先验分支学会从当前观测推理未来感知且动作有用的结构。推理时丢弃后验分支，不进行任何视觉 rollout。

Result: 在六个仿真基准和多样的真实世界任务中，Being-H0.7 达到最先进或相当的性能。

Conclusion: Being-H0.7 结合了世界模型的预测优势与直接VLA策略的效率和可部署性，在不生成未来帧的情况下实现了未来感知推理。

Abstract: Visual-Language-Action models (VLAs) have advanced generalist robot control by mapping multimodal observations and language instructions directly to actions, but sparse action supervision often encourages shortcut mappings rather than representations of dynamics, contact, and task progress. Recent world-action models introduce future prediction through video rollouts, yet pixel-space prediction is a costly and indirect substrate for control, as it may model visual details irrelevant to action generation and introduces substantial training or inference overhead. We present Being-H0.7, a latent world-action model that brings future-aware reasoning into VLA-style policies without generating future frames. Being-H0.7 inserts learnable latent queries between perception and action as a compact reasoning interface, and trains them with a future-informed dual-branch design: a deployable prior branch infers latent states from the current context, while a training-only posterior branch replaces the queries with embeddings from future observations. Jointly aligning the two branches at the latent reasoning space leads the prior branch to reason future-aware, action-useful structure from current observations alone. At inference, Being-H0.7 discards the posterior branch and performs no visual rollout. Experiments across six simulation benchmarks and diverse real-world tasks show that Being-H0.7 achieves state-of-the-art or comparable performance, combining the predictive benefits of world models with the efficiency and deployability of direct VLA policies.

</details>


### [12] [World Model for Robot Learning: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00080)
*Bohan Hou,Gen Li,Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Xinying Guo,Sicong Leng,Haoran Geng,Yanjie Ze,Tatsuya Harada,Philip Torr,Oier Mees,Marc Pollefeys,Zhuang Liu,Jiajun Wu,Pieter Abbeel,Jitendra Malik,Yilun Du,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从机器人学习视角全面综述了世界模型的研究进展，涵盖其与策略的耦合方式、作为强化学习模拟器的应用、视频世界模型的发展历程，以及在导航和自动驾驶中的具体应用，并总结了代表性数据集、基准和评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中世界模型的文献分散于不同的架构、功能角色和具身应用领域，缺乏系统性梳理。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个统一的综述视角。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，从机器人学习角度系统性地梳理世界模型的架构设计、功能角色（策略耦合、模拟仿真、视频生成）及其在导航与自动驾驶等领域的应用，并汇总相关数据集、基准和评估协议。

Result: 揭示了世界模型在策略学习、规划、模拟评估和数据生成等核心环节中的关键作用，梳理了从基于想象生成到可控、结构化、基础模型规模化的视频世界模型演进路径。

Conclusion: 世界模型已成为机器人学习的核心组件，但仍面临主要挑战；本文为预测建模在具身智能体中的应用指明了未来方向，并将通过GitHub仓库持续更新相关资源。

Abstract: World models, which are predictive representations of how environments evolve under actions, have become a central component of robot learning. They support policy learning, planning, simulation, evaluation, data generation, and have advanced rapidly with the rise of foundation models and large-scale video generation. However, the literature remains fragmented across architectures, functional roles, and embodied application domains. To address this gap, we present a comprehensive review of world models from a robot-learning perspective. We examine how world models are coupled with robot policies, how they serve as learned simulators for reinforcement learning and evaluation, and how robotic video world models have progressed from imagination-based generation to controllable, structured, and foundation-scale formulations. We further connect these ideas to navigation and autonomous driving, and summarize representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation protocols. Overall, this survey systematically reviews the rapidly growing literature on world models for robot learning, clarifies key paradigms and applications, and highlights major challenges and future directions for predictive modeling in embodied agents. To facilitate continued access to newly emerging works, benchmarks, and resources, we will maintain and regularly update the accompanying GitHub repository alongside this survey.

</details>


### [13] [Predictive Spatio-Temporal Scene Graphs for Semi-Static Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00121)
*Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz,Charlie Gauthier,Kumaraditya Gupta,Shima Shahfar,Kirsty Ellis,Steven Parkison,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PredictiveGraphs方法，通过在3D场景图中集成Perpetua$^*$贝叶斯滤波器，实现了对半静态环境中物体时空状态的推理和预测，在仿真和真实场景（三周、每两小时变化）中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"时空语义"表征方法缺乏跨时间推理能力，无法捕捉环境中实例的周期性变化模式（如杯子在橱柜-台面-水槽间的日常移动），因而无法预测未来环境状态。

Method: 将Perpetua$^*$贝叶斯滤波器集成到3D场景图（PredictiveGraphs）中，图中节点表示物体，边编码为Perpetua$^*$滤波器以建模时空语义关系，实现贝叶斯推理以预测环境状态。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界（三周、每两小时间隔的半静态变化）动态导航任务中，该方法在预测未来环境状态方面优于基线，且能应对分布偏移。

Conclusion: Perpetua$^*$滤波器与PredictiveGraphs的结合能够有效进行"时间-空间-语义"推理，适用于机器人需要预测周期性变化的半静态环境场景。

Abstract: We have seen tremendous recent progress in our ability to build "spatio-semantic" representations that enable robots to perform complex reasoning across geometry and semantics. However, the vast majority of these methods lack any ability to perform reasoning across time. This is a desirable property in situations where a robot repeatedly observes an environment where instances may change in between observations, but in a structured way. Consider as an example a home environment where the location of a mug typically moves from the cupboard to a countertop to the sink and then back to the cupboard on a daily basis. We should be able to learn this cyclic behavior and use it to predict the state of the mug in the future. In this work, we propose a method that is able to perform this type of tempo-spatio-semantic reasoning. Underpinning the method is a filter, Perpetua$^*$, that performs Bayesian reasoning on the states of the environment that are observed over time. This filter is integrated within a 3D scene graph structure that we call PredictiveGraphs, where nodes represent objects and edges function as Perpetua$^*$ filters encoding spatio-semantic relationships. We validate the method in both simulation and real-world dynamic navigation tasks, where our real world experiments consist of an environment that is undergoing semi-static changes at a bi-hourly frequency over a period of three weeks. In both settings, we demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines in predicting future environment states, even in the presence of distributional shifts.

</details>


### [14] [E$^2$DT: Efficient and Effective Decision Transformer with Experience-Aware Sampling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00159)
*Kaiyan Zhao,Borong Zhang,Yiming Wang,Xingyu Liu,Xuetao Li,Yuyang Chen,Xiaoguang Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出E²DT框架，通过基于k-DPP的质量-多样性联合采样机制，使Decision Transformer能够主动选择高回报、高不确定性、低覆盖度的经验轨迹，在机器人物体操作任务中显著提升样本效率和策略最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Decision Transformer依赖均匀经验回放，缺乏主动探索机制，导致样本效率低、探索受限；而过度探索又会延迟策略收敛。现有方法无法在效率与有效性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出E²DT框架，将DT内部潜在嵌入用于衡量轨迹窗口间的多样性，同时通过RTG分位数、预测不确定性和基于逆频率的阶段覆盖度构建复合质量指标，两者结合形成质量-多样性联合核，通过k-Determinantal Point Process进行经验采样。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人操作基准上，E²DT持续优于先前方法，证明了经验感知采样与策略学习结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 将策略学习与经验感知采样耦合，为鲁棒的长期机器人学习提供了原则性路径。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation, the Decision Transformer (DT) has emerged as an effective framework for addressing long-horizon tasks. However, DT's performance depends heavily on the coverage of collected experiences. Without an active exploration mechanism, standard DT relies on uniform replay, which leads to poor sample efficiency, limited exploration, and reduced overall effectiveness. At the same time, while excessive exploration can help avoid local optima, it often delays policy convergence and leads to degraded efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose E$^2$DT, a DT-guided k-Determinantal Point Process sampling framework that enables the model to actively shape its own experience selection. Our framework is experience-aware, allowing E$^2$DT to be both efficient, by prioritizing sampling quality, such as high-return, high-uncertainty, and underrepresented trajectories, and effective, by ensuring diversity across trajectory windows to preserve policy optimality. Specifically, DT's internal latent embeddings measure diversity across trajectory windows, while quality is quantified through a composite metric that integrates return-to-go (RTG) quantiles, predictive uncertainty, and stage coverage based on inverse frequency. These two dimensions are integrated into a novel quality-diversity joint kernel that prioritizes the most informative experiences, thereby enabling learning that is both efficient and effective. We evaluate E$^2$DT on challenging robotic manipulation benchmarks in both simulation and real-robot settings. Results show that it consistently outperforms prior methods. These findings demonstrate that coupling policy learning with experience-aware sampling provides a principled path toward robust long-horizon robotic learning.

</details>


### [15] [Lucid-XR: An Extended-Reality Data Engine for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00244)
*Yajvan Ravan,Adam Rashid,Alan Yu,Kai McClennen,Gio Huh,Kevin Yang,Zhutian Yang,Qinxi Yu,Xiaolong Wang,Phillip Isola,Ge Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lucid-XR是一个生成式数据引擎，利用基于网页的物理仿真环境和AI视频生成管道，为机器人系统创建多样化、逼真的多模态训练数据，支持零样本迁移到真实环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人训练数据收集方法需要专用设备、耗时长且场景多样性有限，难以支撑机器人在复杂真实环境中的泛化能力。作者希望构建一个低成本、可扩展、高效的数据生成方案。

Method: 1) 开发了vuer——一个直接运行在XR头显上的基于网页的物理仿真环境，提供无延迟、无需专业设备的沉浸式交互；2) 集成设备端物理仿真与人体到机器人的姿态重定向；3) 采用基于物理引导的视频生成管道，可通过自然语言指令控制数据增强。

Result: 机器人视觉策略在完全使用Lucid-XR合成数据训练后，能够零样本迁移到未见过的、杂乱且光照条件差的真实评估环境中。在涉及软材料、松散颗粒和刚体接触的灵巧操作任务上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: Lucid-XR通过结合在线仿真、姿态重定向和物理引导的视频生成，为机器人训练提供高效、多样化的合成数据方案，显著降低了真实世界部署对大规模真实数据采集的依赖。

Abstract: We introduce Lucid-XR, a generative data engine for creating diverse and realistic-looking multi-modal data to train real-world robotic systems. At the core of Lucid-XR is vuer, a web-based physics simulation environment that runs directly on the XR headset, enabling internet-scale access to immersive, latency-free virtual interactions without requiring specialized equipment. The complete system integrates on-device physics simulation with human-to-robot pose retargeting. Data collected is further amplified by a physics-guided video generation pipeline steerable via natural language specifications. We demonstrate zero-shot transfer of robot visual policies to unseen, cluttered, and badly lit evaluation environments, after training entirely on Lucid-XR's synthetic data. We include examples across dexterous manipulation tasks that involve soft materials, loosely bound particles, and rigid body contact. Project website: https://lucidxr.github.io

</details>


### [16] [Task-Conditioned Uncertainty Costmaps for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00261)
*Kartikeya Singh,Christo Aluckal,Romeo Orsolino,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过建模立足点预测中的认知不确定性，区分分布内（ID）与分布外（OOD）操作区域，将不确定性融入代价地图进行路径规划，在仿真和真实环境中实现最高37%的可行性误差降低，并提升规划可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度非结构化地形上，基于感知输入（如高程扫描）的落脚点预测难以保持准确，尤其是在重复步态周期下。现有方法缺乏对训练数据覆盖不足所导致的不确定性的建模能力。

Method: 基于地形观测和指令运动，对预测落脚点中的认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）进行建模，区分ID与OOD操作区域；将不确定性纳入统一代价地图生成框架，实现不确定性感知的路径规划。

Result: 在仿真中OOD检测能力提升，可行性误差降低最高37%；在仿真和真实环境中均表现出比仅基于几何信息的基线方法更可靠的规划行为。

Conclusion: 对预测落脚点中的认知不确定性进行建模，能够有效区分ID与OOD区域，使单一模型在有限数据分布下表达不确定性，并显著提升非结构化地形上的运动规划可行性。

Abstract: Legged robots maintain dynamic feasibility through multicontact interactions with terrain. Learned foothold prediction can provide feasibility-aware costs for motion planning and path selection, but accurately predicting future contacts from perceptual inputs such as height scans remains challenging on highly unstructured terrain, even with a repetitive gait cycle. In this work, we show that modeling epistemic uncertainty in predicted footholds, conditioned on terrain observations and commanded motion, distinguishes in-distribution from out-of-distribution operating regimes in simulation and real-world settings. This allows a single learned model, trained on limited data distributions, to express uncertainty caused by missing training coverage. We use this learned uncertainty to detect OOD regions and incorporate them into a unified costmap-generation framework for uncertainty-aware path planning. Using these uncertainty-aware costmaps, we evaluate feasibility error across in-distribution and OOD terrains in simulation and real-world settings. The results show improved OOD detection, up to a 37% reduction in simulation feasibility error, and more reliable planning behavior than geometry-only baselines.

</details>


### [17] [A Model-based Visual Contact Localization and Force Sensing System for Compliant Robotic Grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00307)
*Kaiwen Zuo,Shuyuan Yang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的视觉力传感方法，通过腕部RGB-D摄像头提取软体夹爪结构关键点，结合逆有限元分析仿真和迭代接触定位，实现对不同物体的抓取力间接估计，负载阶段RMSE为0.23 N，归一化误差2.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取力估计方法主要依赖端到端深度学习，泛化到新场景时脆弱；而基于模型的方法不适用于抓取任务和现代夹爪几何结构。需要一种兼具泛化能力和物理可解释性的力估计方案。

Method: 1) 从腕部RGB-D图像中提取鳍条状软体夹爪的结构关键点；2) 利用这些关键点定义逆有限元分析（FEA）仿真的参数（在SOFA框架中）；3) 迭代接触定位子系统：基于深度学习的在线3D重建与位姿估计，动态更新接触位置，对视觉遮挡和未知物体具有鲁棒性。

Result: 负载阶段平均RMSE为0.23 N，归一化RMSD为2.11%；整个抓取过程平均RMSE为0.48 N，归一化RMSD为4.34%。

Conclusion: 该方法在多种条件和不同物体下表现良好，展示了实时、基于模型的软体夹爪间接力传感的潜力，解决了深度学习方法泛化性差和传统模型方法不适用于抓取的问题。

Abstract: Grasp force estimation can help prevent robots from damaging delicate objects during manipulation and improve learning-based robotic control. Integrating force sensing into deformable grippers negotiates trade-offs in cost, complexity, mechanical robustness, and performance. With the growing integration of RGB-D wrist cameras into robotic systems for control purposes, camera-based techniques are a promising solution for indirect visual force estimation. Current approaches mostly utilize end-to-end deep learning, which can be brittle when generalizing to new scenarios, while existing model-based approaches are unsuited to grasping and modern grasper geometries. To address these challenges, we developed a model-based visual force sensing approach integrating an iterative contact localization with generalization to unseen objects. The system extracts structural key points from wrist camera RGB-D images of deforming fin-ray-shaped soft grippers, and uses these key points to define parameters of an inverse finite element analysis simulation in Simulation Open Framework Architecture. The iterative contact localization sub-system utilizes a deep learning-based online 3D reconstruction and pose estimation pipeline to dynamically update contact location, and is robust to visual occlusion and unseen objects. Our system demonstrated an average root mean square error of 0.23 N and normalized root mean square deviation of 2.11% during the load phase, and 0.48 N and 4.34% over the entire grasping process when interacting with different objects under various conditions, showcasing its potential for real-time model-based indirect force sensing of soft grippers.

</details>


### [18] [PrefMoE: Robust Preference Modeling with Mixture-of-Experts Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00384)
*Ziqin Yuan,Ruiqi Wang,Dezhong Zhao,Baijian Yang,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrefMoE提出了一种基于混合专家（MoE）的奖励学习框架，通过多个奖励专家和轨迹级软路由来应对偏好数据中的异构和冲突监督，在D4RL和MetaWorld基准上提升了偏好预测鲁棒性和下游策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好奖励学习方法在面对大规模偏好数据中的标注者间分歧和标注者内部不一致时，通常拟合单一奖励模型，被迫平均不兼容的信号，导致鲁棒性受限。

Method: 提出PrefMoE框架，包含多个专门的奖励专家（experts），通过轨迹级软路由（trajectory-level soft routing）自适应组合不同专家，并引入负载均衡正则化器（load-balancing regularizer）防止专家坍缩，以捕捉异构偏好数据中的多样潜在偏好模式。

Result: 在D4RL的 locomotion 任务和 MetaWorld 的操作任务上，PrefMoE相比强单模型基线在偏好预测鲁棒性和下游策略学习可靠性方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 基于混合专家的奖励学习框架能够有效处理偏好数据中的噪声和异构性，提升奖励建模的鲁棒性并带来更可靠的下游策略学习。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative to manual reward engineering by learning reward structures from comparative feedback. However, large-scale preference datasets, whether collected from crowdsourced annotators or generated by synthetic teachers, often contain heterogeneous and partially conflicting supervision, including disagreement across annotators and inconsistency within annotators. Existing reward learning methods typically fit a single reward model to such data, forcing it to average incompatible signals and thereby limiting robustness. To solve this, we propose PrefMoE, a mixture-of-experts reward learning framework for robust preference modeling. PrefMoE learns multiple specialized reward experts and uses trajectory-level soft routing to combine them adaptively, enabling the model to capture diverse latent preference patterns under noisy and heterogeneous preference supervision. A load-balancing regularizer further stabilizes training by preventing expert collapse. Across locomotion benchmarks from D4RL and manipulation tasks from MetaWorld, PrefMoE improves preference prediction robustness and leads to more reliable downstream policy learning than strong single-model baselines.

</details>


### [19] [MiniVLA-Nav v1: A Multi-Scene Simulation Dataset for Language-Conditioned Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00397)
*Ali Al-Bustami,Jaerock Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiniVLA-Nav v1是一个面向语言条件目标接近导航任务的仿真数据集，包含1,174个episodes，在四种Isaac Sim环境中使用Nova Carter机器人，提供RGB图像、深度图、实例分割掩码及专家动作标签，支持多种评估场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航数据集在语言指令与视觉感知的细粒度对齐、多样化环境覆盖以及专家动作标签标准化方面存在不足，亟需一个高质量、多场景的仿真数据集来推动语言条件导航研究。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Nova Carter差分驱动平台，在四个Isaac Sim环境（办公室、医院、仓库、多层货架仓库）中采集数据。每条指令与640x640 RGB图、深度图、实例分割掩码对齐，并记录60Hz的(v,omega)和7x7 tokenized专家动作。设计了三个生成距离层级（近/中/远）和12个目标类别，使用18个训练模板和12个OOD释义模板，最终划分5个评估子集。

Result: 数据集包含1,174个episodes，生成距离与轨迹长度的Pearson相关系数达0.94，支持分布内准确率评估、模板释义鲁棒性测试及OOD目标类别基准测试。

Conclusion: MiniVLA-Nav v1为语言条件目标接近导航提供了标准化基准数据集，其多环境、多模板和OOD划分设计有助于提升导航模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: We present MiniVLA-Nav v1, a simulation dataset for Language-Conditioned Object Approach (LCOA) navigation: given a short natural-language instruction, an NVIDIA Nova Carter differential-drive robot must navigate to the named object and stop within 1 m across four photorealistic Isaac Sim environments (Office, Hospital, Full Warehouse, and Warehouse with Multiple Shelves). Each of the 1,174 episodes pairs an instruction with synchronized 640x640 RGB images, metric depth maps (float32, metres), and instance segmentation masks, together with continuous (v,omega) and 7x7 tokenized expert action labels recorded at 60 Hz from a vision-based proportional controller. Trajectory diversity is ensured through three spawn-distance tiers (near: 1.5-3.5 m, mid: 3.5-7.0 m, far: global curated points; Pearson r=0.94 between spawn distance and trajectory length), 12 object categories, 18 training templates, and 12 paraphrase-OOD templates. Five evaluation splits support in-distribution accuracy, template-paraphrase robustness, and OOD object-category benchmarking. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/alibustami/miniVLA-Nav

</details>


### [20] [Learning while Deploying: Fleet-Scale Reinforcement Learning for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00416)
*Yi Wang,Xinchen Li,Pengwei Xie,Pu Yang,Buqing Nie,Yunuo Cai,Qinglin Zhang,Chendi Qu,Jeffrey Wu,Jianheng Song,Xinlin Ren,Jingshun Huang,Mingjie Pan,Siyuan Feng,Zhi Chen,Jianlan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LWD框架，使预训练的通用VLA机器人策略能够通过车队级离线到在线强化学习持续后训练，利用部署中的自主 rollout 和人类干预数据优化策略，在16台双臂机器人、8个真实操作任务上达到95%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线预训练的通用机器人策略在真实部署中面临分布偏移、长尾故障、任务变化等挑战，固定演示数据集无法覆盖这些情况，需要一种能在部署过程中持续学习和改进的框架。

Method: 提出Learning While Deploying (LWD)框架，包含：(1) 闭环架构：连接部署、共享物理经验、策略改进和重新部署；(2) Distributional Implicit Value Learning (DIVL) 用于从异构稀疏奖励的车队数据中稳定估计价值；(3) Q-learning via Adjoint Matching (QAM) 用于从基于流的VLA动作生成器中提取策略。

Result: 在16台双臂机器人、8个真实操作任务上验证，通用策略持续提升，平均成功率95%，长时域任务提升最大。

Conclusion: LWD框架有效实现了通用VLA策略在部署过程中的持续学习与改进，为机器人策略的规模化后训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies increasingly benefit from large-scale pretraining, but offline data alone is insufficient for robust real-world deployment. Deployed robots encounter distribution shifts, long-tail failures, task variations, and human correction opportunities that fixed demonstration datasets cannot fully capture. We present Learning While Deploying (LWD), a fleet-scale offline-to-online reinforcement learning framework for continual post-training of generalist Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies. Starting from a pretrained VLA policy, LWD closes the loop between deployment, shared physical experience, policy improvement, and redeployment by using autonomous rollouts and human interventions collected across a robot fleet. To stabilize learning from heterogeneous, sparse-reward fleet data, LWD combines Distributional Implicit Value Learning (DIVL) for robust value estimation with Q-learning via Adjoint Matching (QAM) for policy extraction in flow-based VLA action generators. We validate LWD on a fleet of 16 dual-arm robots across eight real-world manipulation tasks, including semantic grocery restocking and 3--5 minute long-horizon tasks. A single generalist policy improves as fleet experience accumulates, reaching an average success rate of 95%, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [21] [Stereo Multistage Spatial Attention for Real-Time Mobile Manipulation Under Visual Scale Variation and Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00471)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Hyogo Hiruma,Masaki Yoshikawa,Hiroshi Ito,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于立体多级空间注意力的深度预测学习方法，用于实时移动操作。该方法从立体图像中提取任务相关的空间注意力点，通过层级循环架构与机器人状态融合实现闭环动作预测。在四种真实世界移动操作任务（刚性放置、铰接物体操作、可变形物体交互）上验证，在随机初始位置和视觉干扰条件下，相比模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作基线方法，鲁棒性和任务成功率均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界非结构化环境中，机器人依赖机载视觉感知自主移动时，相机视角的连续变化会导致目标物体视觉尺度显著变化，影响基于视觉的运动生成。现有方法对此类视觉尺度变化鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出立体多级空间注意力深度预测学习方法。首先从立体图像中提取任务相关的空间注意力点，然后通过层级循环架构将这些注意力点与机器人状态进行融合，实现闭环动作预测。整体框架结合了立体空间注意力与预测性时序建模。

Result: 在移动操作平台上进行了四种真实世界任务评估（刚性放置、铰接物体操作、可变形物体交互），在随机初始位置和视觉干扰条件下，相比模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作基线方法，任务成功率和鲁棒性均得到提升。

Conclusion: 结构化的立体空间注意力结合预测性时序建模，在所评估的移动操作场景中是一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in open, unstructured real-world environments must rely on onboard visual perception while autonomously moving across different locations. Continuous changes in onboard camera viewpoints cause significant visual scale variations in target objects, affecting vision-based motion generation. In this work, we present a stereo multistage spatial attention-based deep predictive learning method for real-time mobile manipulation. The proposed methods extracts task-relevant spatial attention points from stereo images and integrates them with robot states through a hierarchical recurrent architecture for closed-loop action prediction. We evaluate the system on four real-world mobile manipulation tasks using a mobile manipulator, including rigid placement, articulated object manipulation, and deformable object interaction. Experiments under randomized initial positions and visual disturbance conditions demonstrate improved robustness and task success rates compared to representative imitation learning and vision-language-action baselines under identical control settings. The results indicate that structured stereo spatial attention combined with predictive temporal modeling provides an effective solution within the evaluated mobile manipulation scenarios.

</details>


### [22] [MSACT: Multistage Spatial Alignment for Stable Low-Latency Fine Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00475)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Masaki Yoshikawa,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多级空间注意力模块，结合ACT和ResNet视觉先验，通过自监督时序对齐损失抑制注意力漂移，在ALOHA双臂平台上实现低延迟、高稳定的精细操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂精细操作方法在低延迟控制、视觉定位稳定性与数据效率之间存在权衡：ACT等动作分块策略依赖密集视觉特征缺乏空间一致性，扩散策略存在迭代采样延迟，VLA和体素方法计算开销大。需一种在有限演示下兼具低延迟与稳定视觉定位的方法。

Method: 基于ACT框架和预训练ResNet视觉先验，引入多级空间注意力模块提取任务相关的2D注意力点作为局部空间模态用于动作预测；设计自监督时序对齐损失，将预测的注意力序列与未来帧视觉特征对齐，无需关键点标注即可抑制漂移。

Result: 在ALOHA双臂平台的仿真和真实精细操作任务中，该方法在任务成功率、注意力漂移、推理延迟和视觉扰动鲁棒性方面均取得改进，在保持低延迟推理的同时提升了定位稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了有限演示下视觉到动作映射的稳定性问题，为双臂精细操作提供了低延迟、高鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world fine manipulation, particularly in bimanual manipulation, typically requires low-latency control and stable visual localization, while collecting large-scale data is costly and limited demonstrations may lead to localization drift. Existing approaches make different trade-offs: action-chunking policies such as ACT enable low-latency execution and data efficiency but rely on dense visual features without explicit spatial consistency, generative methods such as Diffusion Policy improve expressiveness but can incur iterative sampling latency, vision-language-action and voxel-based methods enhance generalization and geometric grounding but require higher computational cost and system complexity. We introduce a multistage spatial attention module that extracts stable 2D attention points and jointly predicts future attention sequences with a temporal alignment loss. Built upon ACT with a pretrained ResNet visual prior, a multistage attention module extracts task-relevant 2D attention points as a local spatial modality for action prediction. To maintain consistent object tracking, we introduce a self-supervised objective that aligns predicted attention sequences with visual features from future frames, suppressing drift without keypoint annotations and improving stability of the vision-to-action mapping under limited data. Experiments on simulated and real-world fine manipulation tasks, conducted on the ALOHA bimanual platform, evaluate task success, attention drift, inference latency, and robustness to visual disturbances. Results indicate improvements in localization stability and task performance while maintaining low-latency inference under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [23] [Recovering Hidden Reward in Diffusion-Based Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00623)
*Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Yuting Hu,Shaokai Wu,Wenyuan Xie,Guodong Zhang,Qicheng He,Deyi Ji,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EnergyFlow框架，通过参数化标量能量函数（其梯度为去噪场）统一了生成式动作建模与逆强化学习。理论上证明了在最大熵最优性下，去噪分数匹配学习的分数函数能恢复专家soft Q函数的梯度，从而无需对抗训练即可提取奖励；同时证明保守场约束降低了假设复杂度并收紧OOD泛化边界。实验表明EnergyFlow在多种操作任务上达到SOTA模仿性能，其奖励信号优于对抗IRL和基于似然的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法主要分为两类：生成式动作建模（如扩散策略）和逆强化学习，两者在目标函数和训练方式上存在差异。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，探索在统一框架下同时实现高质量的动作生成和有效的奖励函数提取，并利用结构约束作为有利的归纳偏置来提升策略泛化能力。

Method: EnergyFlow将能量函数参数化，其梯度作为去噪场。通过去噪分数匹配学习分数函数，在最大熵最优性假设下证明该分数函数恢复专家soft Q函数的梯度，从而实现免对抗训练的奖励提取。理论上证明保守场约束（即梯度场为某个势函数的梯度）降低了假设复杂度并收紧OOD泛化边界，同时刻画了恢复奖励的可辨识性以及分数估计误差如何传播到动作偏好。实验在多种操作任务上进行验证。

Result: 在多种操作任务上，EnergyFlow在模仿性能上达到SOTA水平；其提取的奖励信号用于下游强化学习时，优于对抗性IRL方法和基于似然的方法。

Conclusion: 有效奖励提取所需的结构约束（保守场）同时作为有利的归纳偏置提升了策略泛化能力，表明生成式建模与IRL的统一是富有前景的方向。

Abstract: This paper introduces EnergyFlow, a framework that unifies generative action modeling with inverse reinforcement learning by parameterizing a scalar energy function whose gradient is the denoising field. We establish that under maximum-entropy optimality, the score function learned via denoising score matching recovers the gradient of the expert's soft Q-function, enabling reward extraction without adversarial training. Formally, we prove that constraining the learned field to be conservative reduces hypothesis complexity and tightens out-of-distribution generalization bounds. We further characterize the identifiability of recovered rewards and bound how score estimation errors propagate to action preferences. Empirically, EnergyFlow achieves state-of-the-art imitation performance on various manipulation tasks while providing an effective reward signal for downstream reinforcement learning that outperforms both adversarial IRL methods and likelihood-based alternatives. These results show that the structural constraints required for valid reward extraction simultaneously serve as beneficial inductive biases for policy generalization. The code is available at https://github.com/sotaagi/EnergyFlow.

</details>


### [24] [Paired-CSLiDAR: Height-Stratified Registration for Cross-Source Aerial-Ground LiDAR Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00634)
*Montana Hoover,Jing Liang,Tianrui Guan,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Paired-CSLiDAR跨源空地LiDAR基准数据集，用于单扫描位姿精化，并设计了无训练的几何驱动精化管线RGSR，通过高度分层ICP、反向配准和置信门控选择机制，在基准上达到86.0%的S@0.75m和99.8%的S@1.0m，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地LiDAR扫描模态差异大（航拍获取屋顶和冠层，地面获取立面与冠下），两者仅共享部分几何（主要是地形表面），导致标准配准方法和学习型对应模型易收敛到度量错误的局部最小值。现有基准和方法缺乏针对单扫描空地位姿精化的高质量数据集和有效方案。

Method: 提出Residual-Guided Stratified Registration (RGSR)：1) 高度分层ICP（按高度分层进行ICP配准），2) 反向配准方向，3) 置信门控的accept-if-better选择策略。此外，结合Fourier-Mellin BEV proposals处理极端部分重叠场景。

Result: 在包含9,012扫描的主基准上，RGSR达到86.0% S@0.75m和99.8% S@1.0m，优于confidence-gated cascade（83.7%）和GeoTransformer（76.3%）。通过独立测量控制和轨迹一致性验证了基于RMSE的位姿选择有效性。

Conclusion: RGSR作为无训练、仅几何的精化管线，有效利用共享地面平面解决了空地配准难题；RMSE-based位姿选择得到独立验证；Fourier-Mellin BEV proposals在极端部分重叠下可能降低RMSE但增加实际位姿误差。

Abstract: We introduce Paired-CSLiDAR (CSLiDAR), a cross-source aerial-ground LiDAR benchmark for single-scan pose refinement: refining a ground-scan pose within a 50 m-radius aerial crop. The benchmark contains 12,683 ground-aerial pairs across 6 evaluation sites and per-scan reference 6-DoF alignments for sub-meter root-mean-square error (RMSE) evaluation. Because aerial scans capture rooftops and canopy while ground scans capture facades and under-canopy, the two modalities share only a fraction of their geometry, primarily the terrain surface, causing standard registration methods and learned correspondence models to converge to metrically incorrect local minima. We propose Residual-Guided Stratified Registration (RGSR), a training-free, geometry-only refinement pipeline that exploits the shared ground plane through height-stratified ICP, reversed registration directions, and confidence-gated accept-if-better selection. RGSR achieves 86.0% S@0.75 m and 99.8% S@1.0 m on the primary benchmark of 9,012 scans, outperforming both the confidence-gated cascade at 83.7% and GeoTransformer at 76.3%. We validate RMSE-based pose selection with independent survey control and trajectory consistency, and show that added Fourier-Mellin BEV proposals can reduce RMSE while increasing actual pose error under extreme partial overlap. The dataset and code are being prepared for public release.

</details>


### [25] [Affordance Agent Harness: Verification-Gated Skill Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00663)
*Haojian Huang,Jiahao Shi,Yinchuan Li,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Affordance Agent Harness，一个针对开放世界场景中可操作区域接地（affordance grounding）的闭环运行时系统。该系统通过统一异构技能、检索情景记忆、使用Router自适应选择技能、Verifier门控决策及最终融合判断，在多个基准上实现了比固定流水线更优的精度-成本帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可操作区域接地系统通常采用固定流水线组合多技能（检测、分割、交互想象），但存在三个关键缺陷：1) 固定流水线与每个实例的难度不匹配；2) 中间错误缺乏定向恢复机制；3) 无法复用重复物体的经验。这些问题本质上是系统层面的问题：测试时的接地需要获取正确证据、判断证据是否足够可靠、并在有限推理成本和无标签条件下做出决策。

Method: 提出Affordance Agent Harness，包含以下核心组件：1) 证据存储与成本控制的闭环运行时；2) 检索情景记忆为重复类别提供先验；3) Router自适应选择和参数化技能；4) 可操作区域专用的Verifier通过自一致性、跨尺度稳定性和证据充分性门控决策，触发定向重试；5) 最终判断器融合累积证据和轨迹进行预测。

Result: 在多个可操作区域基准测试和难度控制子集上，该方法相比固定流水线基线实现了更优的精度-成本帕累托前沿，在提升接地质量的同时减少了平均技能调用次数和延迟。

Conclusion: 通过自适应、闭环的证据获取与决策机制，而非固定流水线，可以有效解决开放世界中可操作区域接地面临的系统级难题，在推理效率与准确性之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: Affordance grounding requires identifying where and how an agent should interact in open-world scenes, where actionable regions are often small, occluded, reflective, and visually ambiguous. Recent systems therefore combine multiple skills (e.g., detection, segmentation, interaction-imagination), yet most orchestrate them with fixed pipelines that are poorly matched to per-instance difficulty, offer limited targeted recovery from intermediate errors, and fail to reuse experience from recurring objects. These failures expose a systems problem: test-time grounding must acquire the right evidence, decide whether that evidence is reliable enough to commit, and do so under bounded inference cost without access to labels. We propose Affordance Agent Harness, a closed-loop runtime that unifies heterogeneous skills with an evidence store and cost control, retrieves episodic memories to provide priors for recurring categories, and employs a Router to adaptively select and parameterize skills. An affordance-specific Verifier then gates commitments using self-consistency, cross-scale stability, and evidence sufficiency, triggering targeted retries before a final judge fuses accumulated evidence and trajectories into the prediction. Experiments on multiple affordance benchmarks and difficulty-controlled subsets show a stronger accuracy-cost Pareto frontier than fixed-pipeline baselines, improving grounding quality while reducing average skill calls and latency. Project page: https://tenplusgood.github.io/a-harness-page/.

</details>

### [26] [Smart Profit-Aware Crop Advisory System: Kisan AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00133)
*Debasis Dwibedy,Avyay Nishtala,Pranathi Mukku,D Snehaja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Kisan AI是一个利润感知的智能农作物咨询系统，通过在传统农业特征中引入市场价格变量，使用随机森林模型实现99.3%的准确率，并结合价格预测、病害检测和多语言AI聊天机器人，构建了面向印度农民的完整移动端平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农作物咨询系统存在"经济盲目性"，仅优化生物产量而忽略市场价格，导致农民做出农学上合理但经济上不可行的决策。

Method: 在七个标准农业属性基础上增加市场价格特征，构建九特征数据集；使用随机森林分类器训练并与八个基线模型对比；将RF模型集成到多语言渐进式Web应用中，结合Facebook Prophet六个月价格预测引擎、MobileNetV2病害检测模块以及基于Anthropic Claude API的九语言AI聊天机器人。

Result: 随机森林模型达到99.3%的最高准确率和最低对数损失，证明市场价格作为预测特征既有效又有影响力。

Conclusion: Kisan AI通过将市场价格纳入决策框架，解决了传统咨询系统的经济盲目性问题，为农民提供了一个可安装于移动设备的综合利润感知平台。

Abstract: Modern crop advisory systems exhibit a critical limitation termed \textit{economic blindness}. These systems primarily optimize for biological yield, often overlooking market price, which can lead farmers toward agronomically sound yet financially unviable decisions. In this paper, we develop Kisan AI, a smart profit-aware crop advisory system that resolves the above-mentioned limitation through a research-driven, full-stack application. We train the Random Forest(RF) classifier model on a nine-feature benchmark dataset, the standard seven agronomic attributes augmented with a \textit{market\_price} variable, and evaluated against eight baseline models, considering the evaluation matrices, such as, accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and Log Loss. The RF model achieves the highest accuracy of 99.3\% and the lowest Log Loss, confirming that the inclusion of market price as a predictive feature is both valid and impactful. We then implement the RF model within a multilingual progressive Web App alongside a Facebook Prophet six-month price forecasting engine and a MobileNetV2 disease detection module. A nine-language AI chatbot powered by the Anthropic Claude API unifies all modules into a single, mobile-installable platform accessible to farmers across India.

</details>


### [27] [FedACT: Concurrent Federated Intelligence across Heterogeneous Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00011)
*Md Sirajul Islam,Isabelle G Chapman,N I Md Ashafuddula,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng,Klara Nahrstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedACT是一种面向多联邦学习（FL）任务并发场景下的异构资源感知设备调度方法，通过对齐评分机制和参与公平性策略，动态分配异构设备到多个FL任务，以最小化平均任务完成时间（JCT）并提升模型准确率。实验表明，与最先进基线相比，FedACT可将平均JCT降低最高8.3倍，模型准确率提升最高44.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习研究主要聚焦于单一任务优化，而实际场景中多个FL任务需同时在共享设备池上训练。简单套用单FL优化技术会导致系统性能次优，尤其受设备异构性和资源低效问题影响。因此，需要一种面向多FL并发场景的异构资源感知调度方法。

Method: 提出FedACT调度方法：1) 基于对齐评分机制评估设备可用资源与任务资源需求的兼容性；2) 引入参与公平性策略确保设备在各任务间贡献均衡；3) 制定优先分配高对齐评分设备的优化调度方案。

Result: 在多种FL任务和基准数据集上的综合实验表明，与最先进基线相比，FedACT将平均JCT降低最高8.3倍，模型准确率提升最高44.5%。

Conclusion: FedACT通过资源对齐评分与参与公平性相结合的方式，有效解决了多FL并发场景下的设备异构性和资源低效问题，显著提升了系统性能。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative intelligence across decentralized data source devices in a privacy-preserving way. While substantial research attention has been drawn to optimizing the learning process for an individual task, real-world applications increasingly require multiple machine learning tasks simultaneously training their models across a shared pool of devices. Naively applying single-FL optimization techniques in multi-FL systems results in suboptimal system performance, particularly due to device heterogeneity and resource inefficiency. To address such a critical open challenge, we introduce {\em FedACT}, a novel resource heterogeneity-aware device scheduling approach designed to efficiently schedule heterogeneous devices across multiple concurrent FL jobs, with the goal of minimizing their average job completion time (JCT). {\em FedACT} dynamically assigns devices to FL jobs based on an alignment scoring mechanism that evaluates the compatibility between available resources of devices and resource demands of jobs. Additionally, it incorporates participation fairness to ensure balanced contributions from devices across jobs, further enhancing the accuracy levels of learned global models. An optimal scheduling plan is formulated in {\em FedACT} by prioritizing devices with higher alignment scores, while ensuring fair participation across jobs. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed scheduling algorithm, we carried out comprehensive experiments using diverse FL jobs and benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that {\em FedACT} reduces the average JCT by up to 8.3\(\times\) and improves model accuracy by up to 44.5\%, compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [28] [What Physics do Data-Driven MoCap-to-Radar Models Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00018)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物理的可解释性框架，通过两个互补指标评估数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型是否真正学习了底层物理规律，发现低重建误差不代表物理一致性，并揭示时间注意力对Transformer模型学习物理规律至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的MoCap-to-radar模型能够生成合理的微多普勒谱图，但研究者质疑这些模型是否真正学习了背后的物理原理，而非仅拟合数据分布。因此需要一种可解释性方法来判断模型是否具备物理一致性。

Method: 提出两个互补的物理可解释性指标：(1) 物理对齐度——衡量模型预测与物理导出的多普勒频率之间的一致性；(2) 速度-频率关系保持度——测试在速度干预下，模型预测是否仍保持速度与频率的物理关系。两个指标仅需MoCap输入和模型预测，无需实测雷达数据。

Result: 实验表明，低重建误差并不保证物理一致性——部分模型重建误差低但在两个物理指标上表现差。进一步分析发现，时间注意力机制是Transformer模型学习物理规律的关键因素。

Conclusion: 重建损失不足以评估模型对物理原理的学习程度，所提出的物理可解释性框架能有效区分模型的物理一致性水平；时间注意力结构对物理规律的学习具有关键作用。

Abstract: Data-driven MoCap-to-radar models generate plausible micro-Doppler spectrograms, but do they actually learn the underlying physics? We introduce a physics-based interpretability framework to answer this question via two proposed complementary metrics: one measures alignment between model predictions and the physics-derived Doppler frequency, while the other tests whether predictions preserve the velocity-frequency relationship under velocity intervention. Both metrics require only MoCap input and model predictions, without access to measured radar data. Experiments across several model architectures reveal that low reconstruction error does not guarantee physical consistency: some, but not all, models achieve low error yet perform poorly on the two physics-based metrics. Further analysis shows that temporal attention is critical for transformer-based models to learn the underlying physics.

</details>


### [29] [AirFM-DDA: Air-Interface Foundation Model in the Delay-Doppler-Angle Domain for AI-Native 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00020)
*Kejia Bian,Meixia Tao,Jianhua Mo,Zhiyong Chen,Leyan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AirFM-DDA，一种在延迟-多普勒-角度（DDA）域运行的空中接口基础模型，通过将CSI从空时频域重参数化到DDA域以显式解耦多径分量，并采用窗口注意力机制配合帧结构感知位置编码（FS-PE），在信道预测和估计任务上实现零样本泛化，同时将训练和推理成本降低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线基础模型在空时频（STF）域处理信道状态信息（CSI），多径分量固有地叠加且结构纠缠，阻碍了通用信道表示学习；同时全局注意力机制带来高昂计算开销。

Method: 将CSI从STF域重参数化到DDA域以显式解耦多径分量；采用窗口注意力模块配合帧结构感知位置编码（FS-PE），利用局部多径依赖聚类特性避免二次复杂度全局注意力，并注入帧结构先验知识。

Result: 在未见场景和数据集上实现优越的零样本泛化，在信道预测和估计任务上持续优于基线；窗口注意力相比全局注意力将训练和推理成本降低近一个数量级；在高移动性、大延迟扩展、严重噪声和极端混叠条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DDA域表征结合窗口注意力机制能有效解耦多径分量并降低计算复杂度，为6G物理层AI-native设计提供了可行的基础模型范式。

Abstract: The success of large foundation models is catalyzing a new paradigm for AI-native 6G network design: wireless foundation models for physical layer design. However, existing models often operate on channel state information (CSI) in the space-time-frequency (STF) domain, where distinct multipath components are inherently superimposed and structurally entangled. This hinders the learning of universal channel representation. Meanwhile, their reliance on global attention mechanisms incurs prohibitive computational overhead. In this paper, we propose AirFM-DDA, an Air-interface Foundation Model operating in the Delay-Doppler-Angle (DDA) domain for physicallayer tasks. Specifically, AirFM-DDA reparameterizes CSI from the STF domain into the DDA domain to explicitly resolve multipath components along physically meaningful axes. It employs a window-based attention module augmented with framestructure-aware positional encoding (FS-PE). This window-based attention aligns with locally clustered multipath dependencies while avoiding quadratic-complexity global attention, and FS-PE injects frame-structure priors into network. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AirFM-DDA achieves superior zero-shot generalization across unseen scenarios and datasets, consistently outperforming the baselines on channel prediction and estimation tasks. Compared to the global attention, its window-based attention reduces training and inference costs by nearly an order of magnitude. Moreover, AirFM-DDA maintains robustness under high mobility, large delay spreads, severe noise, and extreme aliasing conditions.

</details>


### [30] [Learning physically grounded traffic accident reconstruction from public accident reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00050)
*Yanchen Guan,Haicheng Liao,Chengyue Wang,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个从公开交通事故报告中进行参数化多模态事故重建的框架，构建了包含6,217个真实事故案例的CISS-REC数据集，通过语义理解、路径重建和几何推理实现可验证的事故重建，性能优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故通常以文本报告形式记录，但物理上准确的事故重建因缺乏详细的场景测量数据和专家重建结果而困难重重——这些数据稀缺、昂贵且难以大规模获取。现有方法难以从公开可得的报告中进行可扩展的定量事故重建。

Method: 将事故重建形式化为参数化多模态学习问题；构建CISS-REC数据集（6,217例真实事故，来自NHTSA CISS）；提出重建框架：1）将报告语义映射到道路拓扑和参与者属性；2）重建符合车道约束的碰撞前运动轨迹；3）通过局部几何推理和时间分配优化碰撞相关的交互行为。

Result: 在CISS-REC数据集上，该方法在事故点精度和碰撞一致性等指标上优于多个代表性基线方法，实现了最优的整体重建保真度。

Conclusion: 公开事故报告可以作为可验证事故重建的可扩展计算素材，对交通安全分析、仿真和自动驾驶研究具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Traffic accidents are routinely documented in textual reports, yet physically grounded accident reconstruction remains difficult because detailed scene measurements and expert reconstructions are scarce, costly and hard to scale. Here we formulate accident reconstruction from publicly accessible reports and scene measurements as a parameterized multimodal learning problem. We construct CISS-REC, a dataset of 6,217 real-world accident cases curated from the NHTSA Crash Investigation Sampling System, and develop a reconstruction framework that grounds report semantics to road topology and participant attributes, reconstructs lane consistent pre-impact motion, and refines collision relevant interactions through localized geometric reasoning and temporal allocation. Our method outperforms representative baselines on CISS-REC, achieving the strongest overall reconstruction fidelity, including improved accident point accuracy and collision consistency. These results show that public accident reports can serve as scalable computational substrates for quantitatively verifiable accident reconstruction, with potential value for traffic safety analysis, simulation and autonomous driving research.

</details>


### [31] [Smart Ensemble Learning Framework for Predicting Groundwater Heavy Metal Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00056)
*T. Ansah-Narh,G. Y. Afrifa,J. B. Tandoh,K. Asare,M. Addi,K. E. Yorke,D. M. A. Akpoley,K. Aidoo,S. K. Fosuhene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对Densu盆地地下水重金属污染指数（HPI）的统计复杂性和空间异质性，构建了一个集成响应变换与嵌套交叉验证集成机器学习（高斯Copula变换+堆叠Lasso集成，R²=0.96）的预测框架，并通过DBSCAN聚类识别出Fe和Mn是主要污染贡献因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法无法捕捉地下水重金属污染指标的统计复杂性和空间异质性，且HPI通常呈偏态分布并受相关污染物影响，导致不经变换的预测产生偏差。

Method: 对HPI应用三种变换（原始、对数、高斯Copula），在六种学习器（SVM、k-NN、CART、Elastic Net、核岭回归、堆叠Lasso集成）上采用嵌套交叉验证进行评估，并结合DBSCAN聚类进行诊断。

Result: 高斯Copula变换+堆叠集成取得最优结果（R²=0.96，RMSE=0.19），对数变换表现次之（SVM: R²=0.93），原始尺度模型存在过度乐观问题（R²≈1.0）。DBSCAN聚类揭示Fe和Mn是主要HPI贡献因子。

Conclusion: 分布感知的集成学习结合聚类诊断能够为地下水污染提供稳健、可解释的评估，但需注意空间交叉验证不足和流域特异性等局限。

Abstract: Groundwater in the Densu Basin is increasingly threatened by heavy metal contamination, but conventional methods fail to capture the statistical complexity and spatial heterogeneity of pollution indicators. A key challenge is modelling the Heavy Metal Pollution Index (HPI), which is typically skewed and affected by correlated contaminants, leading to biased predictions without transformation. This study develops a predictive framework integrating response transformations with nested cross-validated ensemble machine learning. Three transformations (raw, log, and Gaussian copula) were applied to HPI and evaluated across six learners: support vector regression (SVM), $k$-nearest neighbours (k-NN), CART, Elastic Net, kernel ridge regression, and a stacked Lasso ensemble. Raw-scale models produced deceptively high fits (Elastic Net and stacked ensemble $R^2 \approx 1.0$), suggesting over-optimism. The log transformation stabilised variance (SVM: $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE $= 0.18$; k-NN: $R^2 = 0.92$, RMSE $= 0.20$). The Gaussian copula gave the most reliable results: stacked ensemble $R^2 = 0.96$ (RMSE $= 0.19$), with other learners maintaining high accuracy. Copula-based models improved residuals and produced spatially plausible maps. DBSCAN clustering revealed Fe and Mn as primary HPI contributors, consistent with regional hydrogeochemistry. Limitations include reliance on random (not spatial) cross-validation and basin-specific scope. Future work should explore spatial validation and other geological settings. Overall, distribution-aware ensembles with clustering diagnostics offer robust, interpretable assessments of groundwater contamination.

</details>


### [32] [High-Probability Convergence in Decentralized Stochastic Optimization with Gradient Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00281)
*Aleksandar Armacki,Haoyuan Cai,Ali H. Sayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了去中心化随机优化中结合梯度追踪技术（GT-DSGD）的高概率收敛保证，在放松假设条件下实现了非凸和Polyak-Łojasiewicz损失函数的最优高概率收敛速率，首次为引入偏差校正的去中心化优化方法提供了高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化优化中的高概率收敛结果几乎只关注DSGD算法，且需要强假设（如数据异质性有界、强凸性），这与均方误差下的结果存在差距——后者中引入偏差校正的方法能在放宽假设下收敛且实践性能更好。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 在放松的次高斯噪声条件下，将梯度跟踪（gradient tracking）技术融入DSGD算法，提出GT-DSGD方法，并对其高概率收敛性进行理论分析。

Result: 对于非凸损失函数，GT-DSGD达到了O(log(1/δ)/√(nT))的最优高概率收敛速率；对于Polyak-Łojasiewicz损失函数，达到了O(log(1/δ)/(nT))的最优速率，其中n为智能体数量，T为时间步数，δ∈(0,1)为置信参数。

Conclusion: GT-DSGD在高概率意义下与均方误差意义下具有相同的收敛条件，实现了相当的暂态时间，且偏差校正的优势在高概率意义下同样得以保持。这是去中心化优化中引入偏差校正方法的首个高概率保证。

Abstract: We study high-probability (HP) convergence guarantees in decentralized stochastic optimization, where multiple agents collaborate to jointly train a model over a network. Existing HP results in decentralized settings almost exclusively focus on the Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\mathtt{DSGD}$) algorithm, which requires strong assumptions, such as bounded data heterogeneity, or strong convexity of each agent's cost. This is contrary to the mean-squared error (MSE) results, where methods incorporating bias-correction techniques are known to converge under relaxed assumptions and achieve better practical performance. In this paper we provide the first step toward bridging the gap, by studying HP convergence of $\mathtt{DSGD}$ incorporating the gradient tracking technique, in the presence of noise satisfying a relaxed sub-Gaussian condition. We show that the resulting method, dubbed $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$, achieves order-optimal HP convergence rates for both non-convex and Polyak-Łojasiewicz costs, of order $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{\sqrt{nT}}\Big)$ and $\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{nT}\Big)$, respectively, where $n$ is the number of agents, $T$ is the time horizon and $δ\in (0,1)$ is the confidence parameter. Our results establish that $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ converges in the HP sense under the same conditions on the cost as in the MSE sense, while achieving comparable transient times. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first HP guarantees for decentralized optimization methods incorporating bias-correction. Numerical experiments on real and synthetic data verify our theoretical findings, underlining the superior performance of $\mathtt{GT-DSGD}$ and highlighting that the benefits of incorporating bias-correction are also maintained in the HP sense.

</details>


### [33] [Meritocratic Fairness in Budgeted Combinatorial Multi-armed Bandits via Shapley Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00762)
*Shradha Sharma,Swapnil Dhamal,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出K-SVFair-FBF算法，在完全反馈的预算组合多臂老虎机中通过K-Shapley值实现 meritocratic 公平性，并证明其公平遗憾为O(T^{3/4})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全反馈设置下无法直接获取单个臂的贡献，传统的半反馈方法不适用，因此需要新的公平性框架来估计臂的边际贡献。

Method: 将Shapley值扩展为K-Shapley值（满足对称性、线性、空玩家和效率公理），并设计K-SVFair-FBF算法，通过自适应估计K-Shapley值处理未知估值函数，同时缓解蒙特卡洛近似带来的噪声。

Result: 在公平遗憾上达到O(T^{3/4})的理论界，在联邦学习和社会影响力最大化数据集上优于现有基线。

Conclusion: K-SVFair-FBF有效解决了完全反馈下预算组合老虎机的公平性问题，兼具理论保证和实际效果。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for meritocratic fairness in budgeted combinatorial multi-armed bandits with full-bandit feedback (BCMAB-FBF). Unlike semi-bandit feedback, the contribution of individual arms is not received in full-bandit feedback, making the setting significantly more challenging. To compute arm contributions in BCMAB-FBF, we first extend the Shapley value, a classical solution concept from cooperative game theory, to the $K$-Shapley value, which captures the marginal contribution of an agent restricted to a set of size at most $K$. We show that $K$-Shapley value is a unique solution concept that satisfies Symmetry, Linearity, Null player, and efficiency properties. We next propose K-SVFair-FBF, a fairness-aware bandit algorithm that adaptively estimates $K$-Shapley value with unknown valuation function. Unlike standard bandit literature on full bandit feedback, K-SVFair-FBF not only learns the valuation function under full feedback setting but also mitigates the noise arising from Monte Carlo approximations. Theoretically, we prove that K-SVFair-FBF achieves $O(T^{3/4})$ regret bound on fairness regret. Through experiments on federated learning and social influence maximization datasets, we demonstrate that our approach achieves fairness and performs more effectively than existing baselines.

</details>


### [34] [RunAgent: Interpreting Natural-Language Plans with Constraint-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00798)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RunAgent 是一个多智能体计划执行平台，通过结合自然语言与显式控制结构（如 IF、GOTO、FORALL），实现结构化工作流的逐步执行。它自动推导约束、动态选择推理/工具/代码执行方式，并过滤上下文历史，在 Natural-plan 和 SciBench 数据集上优于基线 LLM 和 PlanGEN 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过执行有针对性的计划来解决问题，但大型语言模型在结构化工作流执行方面仍然不可靠，需要弥合自然语言的表达力与编程的确定性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 RunAgent 平台：1）基于显式控制结构（IF, GOTO, FORALL）的智能体语言；2）根据任务描述和实例自动推导并验证约束；3）动态选择 LLM 推理、工具调用或代码生成执行（如 Python）；4）引入错误纠正机制；5）执行过程中过滤上下文历史，仅保留相关信息。

Result: 在 Natural-plan 和 SciBench 数据集上，RunAgent 的表现优于基线 LLM 和当前最先进的 PlanGEN 方法。

Conclusion: RunAgent 通过将自然语言计划转化为带有显式控制结构和约束验证的可执行流程，有效提升了 LLM 在结构化工作流执行中的可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: Humans solve problems by executing targeted plans, yet large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for structured workflow execution. We propose RunAgent, a multi-agent plan execution platform that interprets natural-language plans while enforcing stepwise execution through constraints and rubrics. RunAgent bridges the expressiveness of natural language with the determinism of programming via an agentic language with explicit control constructs (e.g., \texttt{IF}, \texttt{GOTO}, \texttt{FORALL}). Beyond verifying syntactic and semantic verification of the step output, which is performed based on the specific instruction of each step, RunAgent autonomously derives and validates constraints based on the description of the task and its instance at each step. RunAgent also dynamically selects among LLM-based reasoning, tool usage, and code generation and execution (e.g., in Python), and incorporates error correction mechanisms to ensure correctness. Finally, RunAgent filters the context history by retaining only relevant information during the execution of each step. Evaluations on Natural-plan and SciBench Datasets demonstrate that RunAgent outperforms baseline LLMs and state-of-the-art PlanGEN methods.

</details>

*Edgar Chaillou,Sebastian Rodriguez,Yves Tourbier,Francisco Chinesta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRADIPOR，一种面向汽车碰撞仿真中数值色散预测的工具，通过秩约简自编码器（RRAE）结合监督分类，在常规后处理中识别数值色散敏感区域，无需重复计算，实验表明优于随机森林基线，其中基于斜率的信号表征分类性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车碰撞有限元仿真中，由于并行计算和模型复杂性，仿真结果存在数值色散，需重复计算才能评估，但计算成本过高，因此需要一种无需重复计算即可在常规后处理中预测数值色散的工具。

Method: 采用秩约简自编码器（RRAE）结合监督分类，在碰撞仿真后处理中识别数值色散敏感区域；比较了多种信号表征方式（小波基、斜率等），并与随机森林基线进行对比。

Conclusion: 结构化潜在表征有助于提升汽车碰撞后处理中数值色散检测的效果，支持RRAE作为无重复计算条件下预测数值色散的有效工具。

Abstract: We present CRADIPOR, a numerical dispersion prediction tool for automotive crash simulations. Finite Element (FE) crash models are widely used throughout vehicle development, but their predictions are not strictly repeatable because of parallel computation and model complexity. As a result, performance criteria evaluated during post-processing may exhibit significant numerical dispersion, which complicates engineering decision-making. Although dispersion can be estimated by repeating the same simulation, this approach is generally impractical because of its high computational cost.
  This work therefore investigates a prediction tool that can be applied during routine crash-simulation post-processing without repeating the computation. The proposed approach relies on a Rank Reduction Autoencoder (RRAE) combined with supervised classification in order to identify regions sensitive to numerical dispersion. The comparative analysis suggests that the RRAE-based framework is more effective than the Random Forest baseline on the studied dataset. Among the tested signal representations, wavelet-based and slope-based inputs appear to be the most promising, with slope variations providing the best classification performance. These results support the use of structured latent representations for improving numerical-dispersion detection in automotive crash post-processing.

</details>


### [36] [Hyperspherical Forward-Forward with Prototypical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00082)
*Shalini Sarode,Brian Moser,Joachim Folz,Federico Raue,Tobias Nauen,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超球面前向-前向算法（HFF），通过将每层局部目标从二值拟合重构为超球面特征空间中的直接多类分类问题，解决了原始FF算法推理过程计算瓶颈，实现了单次前向传播完成权重更新和推理，速度提升40倍以上，并在ImageNet-1k上首次突破25% top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Forward-Forward算法在训练时高效，但推理过程需要为每个类别单独执行前向传播，计算开销极大，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 将每层局部学习目标从二值 goodness-of-fit 任务重构为超球面特征空间中的直接多类分类问题，学习一组类别特定的单位范数原型作为几何锚点和隐式负样本，使得权重更新和推理可在单次前向传播中完成。

Result: 推理速度比原始FF算法快40倍以上；在ImageNet-1k上首次实现超过25% top-1准确率（贪心局部学习方法中首次）；迁移学习达到65.96%准确率。

Conclusion: HFF简单易实现，可有效扩展至现代卷积架构，在标准图像分类基准上取得接近反向传播的准确率，为贪心局部学习方法的规模化应用提供了可行性证明。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm presents a compelling, bio-inspired alternative to backpropagation. However, while efficient in training, it has a computationally prohibitive inference process that requires a separate forward pass for every class that is evaluated. In this work, we introduce the Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF), a novel reformulation that resolves this critical bottleneck. Our core innovation is to reframe the local objective of each layer from a binary goodness-of-fit task to a direct multi-class classification problem within a hyperspherical feature space. We achieve this by learning a set of class-specific, unit-norm prototypes that act as geometric anchors and implicit negatives. This architectural innovation preserves the benefits of local training while enabling weight update and inference in a single forward pass, making it >40x faster than the original FF algorithm. Our method is simple to implement, scales effectively to modern convolutional architectures, and achieves superior accuracy on standard image classification benchmarks, closing the gap with backpropagation. Most notably, we are among the first greedy local-learning methods to report over 25% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1k, and 65.96% with transfer learning.

</details>

*Daniel Azenkot,Michael Fire,Eran Ben Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合空间聚类与多维特征分析的公交客流预测框架，通过将城市划分为独立区域并为每个区域训练局部预测模型，在保持与全局模型相当精度的同时，实现了对城市公共交通系统的精细化预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测模型将整个城市视为同质化区域，无法捕捉不同城市区域的独特且局部化的动态特征，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 利用公交客流量数据（路线、时间、站点）及多种开源数据（空间特征、气象条件、时间模式），基于邻近站点共享相似客流特征的原则对城市区域进行空间聚类，然后为每个聚类区域分别训练局部预测模型。

Result: 局部化预测模型达到了与全局模型相当的预测精度。

Conclusion: 空间感知的局部化建模策略对公共交通预测是有效的，为实现更具针对性和高效的服务改进铺平了道路。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of bus ridership (passengers numbers) is crucial for efficient management and optimization of public transport systems.
