Particular attention is given to the trade-off between predictive performance and model interpretability, highlighting the challenges introduced by machine learning approaches. To our knowledge, this is the most comprehensive study on simultaneous modeling of multiple organ-specific PAMPA membranes to date, offering novel insights into membrane-specific permeability profiles.
  We found that expert-designed physico-chemical property descriptors are more fitting for a limited sample size permeabilty study than deep learning based representations.

</details>


### [78] [Scale-Aware Adversarial Analysis: A Diagnostic for Generative AI in Multiscale Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00510)
*Mengke Zhao,Guang-Xing Li,Duo Xu,Keping Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于约束扩散分解（CDD）的诊断框架，用于评估深度学习模型是否真正内化了多尺度物理系统的连续动力学规律，而非仅仅学习离散统计相关性。通过在DDPM模型上应用该方法，发现未受约束的生成模型在面对物理扰动时表现出局部结构冻结和非线性不稳定性，无法维持跨尺度连续性，从而揭示了现有模型在多尺度物理因果推理上的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法（如逐像素扰动、梯度显著性）在评估物理系统模型时，会产生非物理伪影并使输入偏离有效经验分布，无法判断模型是内化了物理规律还是仅插值统计相关性。因此需要一个能进行物理约束数据生成和尺度感知评估的诊断框架。

Method: 提出约束扩散分解（CDD）框架，一种基于扩散的多尺度数据分解算法，可在连续尺度空间内进行物理约束的确定性干预。将该框架应用于DDPM生成模型，通过CDD尺度空间进行扰动，观察模型的响应行为。

Result: 在适度物理扰动下，未约束的生成模型表现出局部结构冻结和非线性不稳定性，而非连续的PDE式响应；模型无法维持跨尺度连续性，生成轨迹在面对未见过的物理状态时发生发散。

Conclusion: 该尺度感知的诊断方法通过合成物理一致的状态连续体，建立了评估算法脆弱性的受控测试场，为未来架构尊重多尺度自然宇宙的因果性提供了必要的物理约束条件。

Abstract: Complex physical systems, from supersonic turbulence to the macroscopic structure of the universe, are governed by continuous multiscale dynamics. While modern machine learning architectures excel at mapping the high-dimensional observables of these systems, it remains unclear whether they internalize the governing physical laws or merely interpolate discrete statistical correlations. Standard Explainable AI (XAI) architectures, particularly perturbation-based and gradient-saliency methods, rely on pixel-wise perturbations, which generate unphysical artifacts and push inputs off the valid empirical distribution. To resolve this, we introduce a diagnostic framework driven by Constrained Diffusion Decomposition (CDD), a diffusion-based multiscale data decomposition algorithm that enables physically constrained data generation and model evaluation via scale-aware modifications. Applying this framework to a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM), we execute deterministic interventions directly within the continuous, CDD-based scale space. We demonstrate that under moderate physical perturbations, the unconstrained generative model exhibits localized structural freezing and non-linear instability rather than continuous PDE-like responses. The network fails to maintain cross-scale continuity, causing the generative trajectory to diverge when pushed into unseen physical states. By synthesizing a continuum of physically coherent states, this scale-informed methodology establishes a controlled test ground to evaluate algorithmic vulnerabilities, providing the rigorous physical constraints necessary for future architectures to respect the multiscale causality of the natural universe.

</details>


### [79] [Hierarchical Abstract Tree for Cross-Document Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00529)
*Ziwen Zhao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ψ-RAG提出了一种无需先验分布假设的层次化抽象树索引结构，并配备多粒度检索智能体，在跨文档多跳问答任务上显著优于RAPTOR和HippoRAG 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于树的RAG方法在处理跨文档多跳问题时面临三大挑战：1) k-means聚类因刚性分布假设引入噪声，适应性差；2) 树索引缺乏显式的跨文档连接，结构孤立；3) 粗粒度抽象丢失了细粒度细节。

Method: 提出Ψ-RAG框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 通过迭代"合并与坍缩"过程构建层次化抽象树索引，无需先验假设即可自适应数据分布；2) 多粒度检索智能体，通过重组查询和基于智能体的混合检索器与知识库智能交互。

Result: 在跨文档多跳QA基准上，平均F1得分比RAPTOR高25.9%，比HippoRAG 2高7.4%。

Conclusion: Ψ-RAG通过自适应数据分布的树索引和智能检索机制，有效解决了跨文档多跳问答中的分布适应性、结构隔离和粗粒度抽象问题，支持从token级问答到文档级摘要的多样任务。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enhances large language models with external knowledge, and tree-based RAG organizes documents into hierarchical indexes to support queries at multiple granularities. However, existing Tree-RAG methods designed for single-document retrieval face critical challenges in scaling to cross-document multi-hop questions: (1) poor distribution adaptability, where $k$-means clustering introduces noise due to rigid distribution assumptions; (2) structural isolation, as tree indexes lack explicit cross-document connections; and (3) coarse abstraction, which obscures fine-grained details. To address these limitations, we propose $Ψ$-RAG, a tree-RAG framework with two key components. First, a hierarchical abstract tree index built through an iterative "merging and collapse" process that adapts to data distributions without a priori assumption. Second, a multi-granular retrieval agent that intelligently interacts with the knowledge base with reorganized queries and an agent-powered hybrid retriever. $Ψ$-RAG supports diverse tasks from token-level question answering to document-level summarization. On cross-document multi-hop QA benchmarks, it outperforms RAPTOR by 25.9% and HippoRAG 2 by 7.4% in average F1 score. Code is available at https://github.com/Newiz430/Psi-RAG.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Continuity: Simulation-free Reconstruction of Discrete Branching Dynamics from Single-cell Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00545)
*Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Bowen Yang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无约束薛定谔桥（USB）框架，用于从破坏性快照数据中推断包含随机性和非保守质量动力学（如细胞增殖与凋亡）的细胞轨迹，该框架能够以单细胞分辨率建模离散的出生-死亡跳跃事件，并在理论和实验上展现出优于或可比于确定性基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平衡最优传输方法将质量视为连续流体，仅在群体层面进行推理，无法捕捉单细胞分辨率下出生-死亡事件的离散跳跃特性，而这对于理解谱系分支和命运决定至关重要。

Method: 提出无约束薛定谔桥（USB）框架，作为分支薛定谔桥（BSB）问题的可处理解决方案；引入免模拟训练目标，实现对高维多组学数据的有效扩展。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，USB的轨迹重建性能优于或可比于确定性基线方法，并且能够独特地实现单细胞分辨率下出生-死亡动力学的真实离散模拟。

Conclusion: USB为整合随机性和非平衡效应的细胞动力学推断提供了一种有效的免模拟框架，尤其适用于需要单细胞分辨率谱系分析的应用场景。

Abstract: Inferring cellular trajectories from destructive snapshots is complicated by the challenges of stochasticity and non-conservative mass dynamics such as cell proliferation and apoptosis. Existing unbalanced Optimal Transport (OT) methods treat mass as a continuous fluid, performing inference at the population level. However, this macroscopic view often fails to capture the discrete, jump-like nature of birth-death events at single-cell resolution, which is essential for understanding lineage branching and fate decisions. We present Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge (USB), a simulation-free framework for learning underlying dynamics that effectively integrates both stochastic and unbalanced effects which also models the discrete, jump-like birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution. Theoretically, USB provides a tractable solution to the Branching Schrödinger Bridge (BSB) problem, offering a rigorous microscopic interpretation where individual cells undergo both Brownian motion and discrete birth-death jumps. Technically, the method implements an efficient solver by introducing a simulation-free training objective that effectively scales to high-dimensional omics data. Empirically, we demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that USB not only achieves trajectory reconstruction performance better than or comparable to deterministic baselines but also uniquely enables realistic discrete simulation of birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution.

</details>


### [81] [Fairness of Classifiers in the Presence of Constraints between Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00592)
*Martin C. Cooper,Imane Bousdira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"公平解释"的机器学习决策公平性定义，即决策的prime-implicant解释不应包含受保护特征，并研究了考虑特征约束前后对公平性判定的影响，分析了三种不同公平性定义及其计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性定义要求决策不依赖于性别等受保护特征，但当特征之间存在约束时，这些依赖关系可能被隐藏，导致误判。因此需要一种能考虑特征约束的公平性定义。

Method: 提出"公平解释"概念：若决策的prime-implicant理由（考虑特征约束）不包含任何受保护特征，则该决策是公平的。定义了三种公平性：（1）所有解释均公平；（2）存在至少一个公平解释；（3）改变受保护特征不改变结果。通过理论分析研究了这些定义之间的关系及检验的计算复杂度。

Result: 发现忽略特征约束会完全改变对决策公平性的判定，即使受保护与非受保护特征之间无约束。厘清了三种公平性定义之间的蕴含关系，并给出了检验公平性的计算复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 基于prime-implicant的公平解释为机器学习公平性提供了新的理论框架，能够有效处理特征约束导致的公平性误判问题，但不同定义的选择和计算可行性仍需权衡。

Abstract: In Machine Learning, an accepted definition of fairness of a decision taken by a classifier is that it should not depend on protected features, such as gender. Unfortunately, when constraints exist between features, such dependencies can be obscured by the constraints.
  To avoid this problem, we propose that a decision be considered fair if it has a fair explanation. We define a fair explanation as a prime-implicant reason for the decision that does not contain any protected feature (where the constraints are taken into account in the definition of prime-implicant). Surprisingly, ignoring constraints can completely change the fairness of a decision (according to this definition) even in the absence of constraints between protected and unprotected features.
  Three possible definitions of fairness of a classifier are that for all its decisions (1) there are only fair explanations, (2) there is at least one fair explanation, or (3) changing protected features does not change the outcome. We identify the relationships between these different definitions of fairness and study the computational complexity of testing fairness of classifiers.

</details>


### [82] [Possibilistic Predictive Uncertainty for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00600)
*Yao Ni,Jeremie Houssineau,Yew Soon Ong,Piotr Koniusz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPPr框架，利用可能性理论中的狄利克雷近似后验预测，在保持原理严谨性的同时实现了高效且性能优异的 epistemic uncertainty 建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性建模方法面临两难困境：贝叶斯方法原理严谨但计算昂贵，而高效的二阶预测器缺乏严格的推导连接其训练目标与 epistemic uncertainty 量化。

Method: 定义参数上的可能性后验，通过上确界算子将其投影到预测空间，并用可学习的狄利克雷可能性函数近似投影后的后验，形成投影-近似策略，得到具有闭式解的简单训练目标。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，DAPPr相比最先进的证据深度学习方法达到了有竞争力或更优的不确定性量化性能，同时兼具原理严谨性和计算效率。

Conclusion: DAPPr成功解决了贝叶斯方法计算昂贵与高效方法缺乏原理推导之间的矛盾，为不确定性量化提供了一种既原则性又实用的新范式。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve impressive results across diverse applications, yet their overconfidence on unseen inputs necessitates reliable epistemic uncertainty modelling. Existing methods for uncertainty modelling face a fundamental dilemma: Bayesian approaches provide principled estimates but remain computationally prohibitive, while efficient second-order predictors lack rigorous derivations connecting their specific objectives to epistemic uncertainty quantification. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Dirichlet-approximated possibilistic posterior predictions (DAPPr), a principled framework leveraging possibility theory. We define a possibilistic posterior over parameters, projects this posterior to the prediction space via supremum operators, and approximates the projected posterior using learnable Dirichlet possibility functions. This projection-and-approximation strategy yields a simple training objective with closed-form solutions. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive or superior uncertainty quantification performance compared to state-of-the-art evidential deep learning methods while maintaining both principled derivation and computational efficiency. Code will be available at https://github.com/MaxwellYaoNi/DAPPr.

</details>


### [83] [Decouple before Integration: Test-time Synthesis of SFT and RLVR Task Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00610)
*Chaohao Yuan,Chenghao Xiao,Yu Rong,Hong Cheng,Long-Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Decoupled Test-time Synthesis (DoTS)框架，通过任务向量算术在推理时独立合成SFT和RLVR检查点的能力，无需联合训练，仅需约3%的计算成本即可匹配或超越训练式集成方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT扩展知识广度，RLVR增强推理深度，但二者难以有效集成：顺序训练导致灾难性遗忘，联合优化存在严重梯度冲突。作者通过任务向量分析发现二者存在30倍幅度差异、45°符号干扰和异构模块级更新分布三大结构特性，亟需一种解耦的集成方法。

Method: 提出DoTS框架：1）将SFT和RLVR检查点独立训练；2）通过任务向量算术在推理时合成能力，不更新模型参数；3）采用选择性稀疏化与保范数缩放减少干扰；4）在少量无标签查询上使用贝叶斯优化搜索一致性与困惑度帕累托前沿的组合系数。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上匹配或超越训练式SFT-RLVR集成方法，仅需约3%计算成本；应用于更强的后训练检查点时超越SOTA模型，并泛化到域外基准。

Conclusion: DoTS验证了SFT和RLVR可通过推理时合成有效集成，为LLM后训练提供了一种高效、解耦且可泛化的新范式。

Abstract: SFT and RLVR represent two fundamental yet distinct paradigms for LLM post-training, each excelling in distinct dimensions. SFT expands knowledge breadth while RLVR enhances reasoning depth. Yet integrating these complementary strengths remains a formidable challenge. Sequential training can cause catastrophic forgetting, and joint optimization often suffers from severe gradient conflicts. We analyze SFT and RLVR through the lens of task vectors and reveal three structural properties behind these failures: a 30* magnitude disparity, 45* sign interference, and heterogeneous module-wise update distributions. These findings show SFT and RLVR are difficult to integrate directly, but they also suggest that the two paradigms modify partly complementary components of the model. Motivated by these observations, we propose Decoupled Test-time Synthesis (DoTS), a post-hoc framework allows SFT and RLVR checkpoints to be trained independently and synthesizes their capabilities only at inference time via task vector arithmetic, without updating model parameters. To reduce interference, DOTS applies selective sparsification with norm-preserving rescaling. It then uses Bayesian optimization on a small set of unlabeled queries to search for combination coefficients on the Pareto frontier of consistency and perplexity. Empirically, \ours matches or exceeds the performance of training-based SFT--RLVR integration methods across multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks, incurring only $\sim$3\% of the computational cost. When applied to stronger post-trained checkpoints, DOTS surpasses SOTA models and generalizes to out-of-domain benchmarks without re-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/chaohaoyuan/DoTS.

</details>


### [84] [Class Angular Distortion Index for Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00637)
*Kaviru Gunaratne,Stephen Kobourov,Jacob Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出类角度失真指数（CADI），一种基于点三元组内角衡量投影中聚类组织忠实度的新指标，可区分全局与局部降维方法对聚类结构的影响，具有可微性并支持优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降维可视化中聚类可能呈现任意或误导性的相对排列（如t-SNE和UMAP的常见问题），而现有聚类质量指标要么仅衡量聚类可分离性，要么假设原始空间中聚类为球形，无法有效评估投影中聚类间相对布局的忠实度。

Method: CADI利用点三元组（triple）之间的内角（internal angles）来衡量投影对原始空间中聚类组织结构的保留程度，通过角度分布的失真量化聚类间相对布局的忠实度。由于基于角度计算，CADI具有可微性，可直接作为损失函数用于优化。

Result: 在真实和合成数据上展示了现有聚类指标失效但CADI能提供可解释结果的案例，并基于CADI实现了新的降维技术，验证了其可优化性。

Conclusion: CADI是一种新颖的、区分全局与局部结构保持的聚类忠实度指标，可微特性使其不仅能评估降维质量，还能驱动新的降维方法设计。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction (DR) techniques are often characterized by whether they preserve global, high-level structures in the data or local, neighborhood structures. This distinction matters in visualization: global methods can obscure clusters while local methods can over-emphasize them. Yet, even when clusters appear distinct, their relative arrangement in the projection may be arbitrary or misleading, a common issue in techniques such as t-SNE and UMAP. Existing cluster quality metrics either only measure cluster separability or assume spherical, globular clusters in the original space. We introduce the Class Angular Distortion Index (CADI), a metric that uses internal angles among point triples to determine the faithfulness of cluster organization in a projection. We show cases on both real and synthetic data where existing cluster metrics fail, but CADI provides an interpretable result. Since it relies on computing angles, CADI is also differentiable, enabling optimization. We demonstrate this with a CADI-based DR technique.

</details>


### [85] [Unlearning Offline Stochastic Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00638)
*Zichun Ye,Runqi Wang,Xuchuang Wang,Xutong Liu,Shuai Li,Mohammad Hajiesmaili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了离线随机多臂老虎机（MAB）中的机器遗忘问题，提出了基于高斯机制和回滚的两种基础算法及自适应切换策略，并在固定样本和分布两种数据模型下提供了性能保证和理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘工作主要聚焦于无监督/监督学习场景，而序列决策系统中的遗忘问题尚缺乏研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，首次将机器遗忘引入离线随机多臂老虎机这一基础序列决策问题。

Method: 基于两种经典基础算法——高斯机制和回滚，提出自适应切换算法以根据数据场景和隐私约束进行选择，并引入混合过程阐明基线算法背后的原理。研究覆盖单源和多源遗忘场景，并在固定样本模型和分布模型两种数据生成模型下进行分析。

Result: 提供了在上述设置下的性能保证，建立了两种数据集模型下的理论下界。实验验证了预测的权衡关系，并展示了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为离线MAB中的机器遗忘问题提供了理论基础和有效算法，填补了序列决策系统中遗忘研究的空白，为后续工作奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to unlearn data points from a learned model, offering a principled way to process data-deletion requests and mitigate privacy risks without full retraining. Prior work has mainly studied unsupervised / supervised machine unlearning, leaving unlearning for sequential decision-making systems far less understood. We initiate the first study of a foundational sequential decision-making problem: offline stochastic multi-armed bandits (MAB). We formalize the privacy constraint for offline MAB and measure utility by the post-unlearning decision quality. We conduct a systematic study of both single- and multi-source unlearning scenarios under two data-generation models, the fixed-sample model and the distribution model. For these settings, our algorithmic design is built on two canonical base algorithms: Gaussian mechanism and rollback, and we propose adaptive algorithms that switch between them according to the data regime and privacy constraint. We further introduce a mixing procedure that elucidates the rationale behind these baselines. We provide performance guarantees across the above settings and establish lower bounds under both dataset models. Experiments validate the predicted tradeoffs and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [86] [Knowing when to trust machine-learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00640)
*Shams Mehdi,Ilkwon Cho,Olexandr Isayev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROBE是一种后验、架构无关的MLIP不确定性量化方法，通过冻结预训练MLIP的每原子表示并训练一个紧凑判别分类器，将不确定性量化转化为选择性分类问题。该方法在多个评估集和两种MLIP架构上优于集成不一致方法，且随骨干表示表达性增强而表现更好，同时多头自注意力可提供化学可解释的诊断信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于集成的不确定性量化方法在基础模型规模的MLIP上扩展性差，且成员不一致信号与每分子预测误差相关性弱。

Method: 冻结预训练MLIP的每原子表示，训练一个紧凑判别分类器，将不确定性量化转化为选择性分类问题（而非误差回归），输出每次预测的可靠性概率。

Result: 在大规模留出评估集和两种结构不同的MLIP架构上，PROBE作为二值可靠性信号优于集成不一致方法；其性能随骨干表示表达性增强而提升；多头自注意力可无额外计算成本地生成每原子重要性图。

Conclusion: PROBE是后验、架构无关的方法，可直接部署在任何暴露每原子表示的MLIP上，且具有向基础模型规模MLIP扩展的有利轨迹。

Abstract: Prevailing machine-learned interatomic potential (MLIP) uncertainty-quantification methods rely on ensembles of independently trained backbones. These methods scale unfavorably with foundation-scale MLIPs, and their member-disagreement signals correlate weakly with per-molecule prediction error. Here we probe the frozen per-atom representations of a pretrained MLIP with a compact discriminative classifier, recasting MLIP uncertainty quantification as selective classification rather than error regression. The resulting method, PROBE (Post-hoc Reliability frOm Backbone Embeddings), produces a per-prediction reliability probability that monotonically tracks actual error without modification to the underlying model. Across large held-out evaluation sets and two structurally distinct MLIP architectures, PROBE outperforms ensemble disagreement as a binary reliability signal, which strengthens with the expressiveness of the backbone representation, implying a favorable scaling trajectory toward foundation-scale MLIPs. Multi-head self-attention additionally yields per-atom importance maps, providing chemically interpretable diagnostics at no additional computational cost. PROBE is post-hoc and architecture-agnostic, and is directly deployable on any MLIP that exposes per-atom representations.

</details>


### [87] [Learning Multimodal Energy-Based Model with Multimodal Variational Auto-Encoder via MCMC Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00644)
*Jiali Cui,Zhiqiang Lao,Heather Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态能量模型（EBM）的学习框架，通过交织MLE更新与MCMC精炼，利用共享潜变量生成器和联合推断模型分别提供数据空间和潜空间的有效初始状态，实现高质量且连贯的多模态合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态EBM的最大似然学习需要在高维联合数据空间中进行MCMC采样，但噪声初始化的Langevin动力学混合效果差，难以发现模态间的一致关系。而多模态VAE虽能捕获模态间依赖，但其共享潜变量生成器和联合推断模型参数化为单峰高斯（或拉普拉斯）分布，严重限制了其近似复杂多模态数据分布的能力。

Method: 提出一个交织学习框架，将生成器、推断模型和EBM的MLE更新与对应的MCMC精炼过程相互结合。生成器学习产生连贯的多模态样本，为EBM采样提供强初始状态；推断模型学习提供信息丰富的潜变量初始化，用于生成器的后验采样。两者作为互补模型促进EBM的有效采样和学习。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多模态合成质量和连贯性方面显著优于多种基线方法。消融研究和分析验证了所提多模态框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过生成器和推断模型与EBM的协同学习，能够有效解决多模态EBM采样困难的问题，生成更加真实且模态间一致的多模态样本。

Abstract: Energy-based models (EBMs) are a flexible class of deep generative models and are well-suited to capture complex dependencies in multimodal data. However, learning multimodal EBM by maximum likelihood requires Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling in the joint data space, where noise-initialized Langevin dynamics often mixes poorly and fails to discover coherent inter-modal relationships. Multimodal VAEs have made progress in capturing such inter-modal dependencies by introducing a shared latent generator and a joint inference model. However, both the shared latent generator and joint inference model are parameterized as unimodal Gaussian (or Laplace), which severely limits their ability to approximate the complex structure induced by multimodal data. In this work, we study the learning problem of the multimodal EBM, shared latent generator, and joint inference model. We present a learning framework that effectively interweaves their MLE updates with corresponding MCMC refinements in both the data and latent spaces. Specifically, the generator is learned to produce coherent multimodal samples that serve as strong initial states for EBM sampling, while the inference model is learned to provide informative latent initializations for generator posterior sampling. Together, these two models serve as complementary models that enable effective EBM sampling and learning, yielding realistic and coherent multimodal EBM samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior performance for multimodal synthesis quality and coherence compared to various baselines. We conduct various analyses and ablation studies to validate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed multimodal framework.

</details>


### [88] [From Prediction to Practice: A Task-Aware Evaluation Framework for Blood Glucose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00645)
*Alireza Namazi,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向任务的血糖预测评估框架，包含低血糖预警和胰岛素剂量决策两个下游任务。通过在真实临床队列和FDA批准的模拟器上进行评估，发现表现良好的模型在关键高风险场景（如餐后推注后）和干预效应预测中可能严重失效，揭示了预测准确性与任务实用性之间存在持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准聚合指标（如低平均误差）无法反映模型在高风险临床场景中的实际表现，可能掩盖危及患者安全的严重失败，亟需建立与下游任务目标一致的评估方法。

Method: 构建双臂评估框架：1）真实数据臂：使用三个临床队列，以事件级召回率和每患者日假警次数评估低血糖预警性能，并引入"推注后切片"等高风险子集分析；2）干预臂：基于FDA批准的UVA/Padova模拟器，构建事实/反事实配对场景，评估模型对胰岛素计划改变的血糖反应预测能力（方向、幅度、排序），并采用临床成本函数评估剂量选择质量。

Result: 1）整体召回率>0.9的模型在推注后高风险子集中严重失效；2）真实数据预测表现优秀的模型无法正确预测干预效应的方向、幅度或排序；3）这些模型在临床成本函数下选择了较差的胰岛素剂量。

Conclusion: 预测准确性与任务实用性之间存在系统性差距，标准评估不足以确保模型在临床决策支持中的安全性。所提出的双评估框架和公开数据集为血糖预测模型的临床适用性提供了可复现的检验标准。

Abstract: Clinical time-series forecasting is increasingly studied for decision support, yet standard aggregate metrics can obscure whether a model is actually useful for the task it is meant to serve. In safety-critical settings, low average error can coexist with dangerous failures in exactly the high-risk regimes that matter most. We present a task-aware evaluation framework for blood glucose forecasting built around two downstream uses: hypoglycemia early warning and insulin dosing decision support. For early warning, we evaluate on real data from three clinical cohorts using event-level recall and false alarms per patient-day, metrics that reflect operational alarm burden rather than aggregate accuracy. We show that models appearing acceptable overall, with recall above 0.9 on the full test set, can fail badly in the post-bolus slice, where insulin-on-board is elevated and missed warnings carry the greatest clinical consequences. Standard forecasting evaluation, however, does not test whether a model can reason about the effects of actions, a requirement for supporting insulin dosing decisions. We therefore add a second, interventional arm using the FDA-accepted UVA/Padova simulator, where we evaluate whether forecasters can predict glucose responses to altered insulin plans in paired factual/counterfactual scenarios. We show that models that look strong on real-data forecasting often fail to predict the direction, magnitude, or ranking of intervention effects, and choose poor insulin doses when evaluated under a clinically motivated cost. Taken together, the two arms reveal a consistent gap between forecasting accuracy and task-relevant usefulness. We release the benchmark, the standardized preprocessing pipeline for public cohorts, and the simulator-based interventional dataset as a reproducible toolkit.

</details>


### [89] [PEACE: Cross-modal Enhanced Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment for Robust Pediatric Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00647)
*Xinran Liu,Yuwen Li,Hongxiang Gao,Heyang Xu,Jianqing Li,Zongmin Wang,Chengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEACE是一个跨模态对齐框架，通过三轴临床语义分解、标签查询特征提取和课程门控优化，实现成人到儿科心电图的迁移学习。在ZZU-pECG数据集上，零样本、50样本和全微调设置下的AUC分别达到59.39%、79.03%和90.89%，在PTB-XL共享标签空间上达到96.65% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科心电图自动诊断面临两大挑战：成人数据训练的模型存在显著的跨人群不匹配，而儿科标注数据往往稀缺。现有方法难以有效将成人丰富的ECG知识迁移到儿科场景。

Method: 提出PEACE框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 三轴临床语义分解（tri-axial clinical semantic decomposition），将临床语义沿三个维度分解；(2) 标签查询特征提取（label-query feature extraction）；(3) 课程门控优化（curriculum-gated optimization）。利用Gemini生成标签条件语义描述符作为辅助训练监督，推理时仅需ECG输入。

Result: 在ZZU-pECG数据集上，零样本AUC 59.39%，50样本AUC 79.03%，全微调AUC 90.89%；在PTB-XL共享标签空间上达到96.65% AUC。

Conclusion: 结构化的临床语义监督能够改善低资源场景下的成人到儿科ECG迁移学习，但在实际部署前仍需前瞻性临床验证和更明确的年龄感知建模。

Abstract: Automated pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) diagnosis remains challenging because models trained predominantly on adult data suffer from substantial cross-population mismatch, while pediatric labels are often scarce. We present PEACE (Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment via Cross-modal Enhancement), a structured cross-modal alignment framework for adult-to-pediatric ECG transfer. PEACE integrates tri-axial clinical semantic decomposition, label-query feature extraction, and curriculum-gated optimization to align transferable adult ECG representations with pediatric diagnostic targets. Since ZZU-pECG provides no paired clinical reports, we generate label-conditioned semantic descriptors using Gemini with concise clinical prompts and use them only as auxiliary training supervision; inference remains ECG-only. On ZZU-pECG, PEACE achieves 59.39%, 79.03%, and 90.89% AUC under zero-shot, 50-shot, and full fine-tuning settings, respectively, and reaches 96.65% AUC on the shared PTB-XL label space. These results suggest that structured clinical semantic supervision can improve low-resource adult-to-pediatric ECG transfer, while prospective clinical validation and more explicit age-aware modeling remain necessary before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [90] [AdaMeZO: Adam-style Zeroth-Order Optimizer for LLM Fine-tuning Without Maintaining the Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00650)
*Zhijie Cai,Haolong Chen,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaMeZO是一种零阶优化器，通过近似Adam风格的一阶和二阶矩估计（无需存储），在保持低GPU内存的同时加速大模型微调，相比MeZO减少高达70%的前向传播次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的基于反向传播的微调方法需要大量GPU内存；MeZO虽能降低内存需求，但收敛速度慢，因其对损失景观不敏感。Adam能探索损失景观但直接应用会三倍增加内存需求，破坏MeZO的优势。

Method: 提出AdaMeZO，一种零阶优化器，在不维护内存中一阶和二阶矩估计的情况下，利用Adam风格的矩估计来指导优化方向，实现内存高效的同时加速收敛。

Result: 理论分析验证了AdaMeZO的性能；实验表明AdaMeZO优于MeZO，且所需前向传播次数最多减少70%；轨迹可视化证实AdaMeZO能够适应多样化的损失景观。

Conclusion: AdaMeZO在保持零阶优化低内存优势的同时，通过近似Adam的矩估计实现了更快的收敛和更少的计算开销，是一种高效的LLM微调方法。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs is necessary for various dedicated downstream tasks, but classic backpropagation-based fine-tuning methods require substantial GPU memory. To this end, a recent work, MeZO, which relies solely on forward passes to fine-tune LLMs, significantly reduces GPU requirements at the cost of slower convergence due to its indifference to loss landscapes. Standard solutions, such as Adam, explore loss landscapes by estimating the first- and second-order moments and storing them in memory to guide the model's movement through dimensions with lower curvature and vice versa. However, directly applying Adam negates MeZO's advantage as it will triple the memory requirement. In light of this, we propose AdaMeZO, a zeroth-order optimizer that leverages Adam-style first- and second-moment estimates without maintaining them in memory. We present a theoretical analysis of AdaMeZO, corroborated by extensive experiments demonstrating AdaMeZO's performance, showing that AdaMeZO can outperform MeZO while requiring up to $70\%$ fewer forward passes. Trajectory visualizations affirm AdaMeZO's ability to adapt to diverse loss landscapes.

</details>


### [91] [Reinforcement Learning with Markov Risk Measures and Multipattern Risk Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00654)
*Andrzej Ruszczynski,Tiangang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对风险厌恶的有限时域马尔可夫决策问题，引入了一类称为"小批量测度"的马尔可夫相干风险度量，并定义了推广线性系统的"多模式风险厌恶问题"概念。基于这些概念，提出了带多模式Q因子近似的特征基Q学习方法，证明了$\mathcal{O}\big(H^2 N^H \sqrt{K}\big)$的高概率遗憾界，并给出了一个经济化版本以简化策略评估步骤。在随机指派问题和多臂老虎机问题上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险厌恶马尔可夫决策问题的研究中，缺乏一种能够有效结合小批量采样思想与马尔可夫相干风险度量的统一框架，且现有方法的遗憾界分析不够完善。

Method: 1) 引入mini-batch马尔可夫相干风险度量；2) 定义多模式风险厌恶问题以推广线性系统；3) 提出基于特征的多模式Q因子近似Q学习方法；4) 推导高概率遗憾界$\mathcal{O}(H^2 N^H \sqrt{K})$；5) 提出经济化版本以优化策略评估反向步骤。

Result: 取得了$\mathcal{O}\big(H^2 N^H \sqrt{K}\big)$的高概率遗憾界，并在随机指派问题和短时域多臂老虎机问题上通过实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的mini-batch风险度量与多模式框架能有效处理风险厌恶MDP，所设计的Q学习算法具有理论保证，经济化版本在计算效率上有所提升。

Abstract: For a risk-averse finite-horizon Markov Decision Problem, we introduce a special class of Markov coherent risk measures, called mini-batch measures. We also define the class of multipattern risk-averse problems that generalizes the class of linear systems. We use both concepts in a feature-based $Q$-learning method with multipattern $Q$-factor approximation and we prove a high-probability regret bound of $\mathcal{O}\big(H^2 N^H \sqrt{ K}\big)$, where $H$ is the horizon, $N$ is the mini-batch size, and $K$ is the number of episodes. We also propose an economical version of the $Q$-learning method that streamlines the policy evaluation (backward) step. The theoretical results are illustrated on a stochastic assignment problem and a short-horizon multi-armed bandit problem.

</details>


### [92] [Evaluating the Architectural Reasoning Capabilities of LLM Provers via the Obfuscated Natural Number Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00677)
*Lixing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出架构推理概念（在陌生数学领域仅靠局部公理和定义合成形式证明的能力），通过混淆自然数游戏（Obfuscated Natural Number Game）基准测试评估大语言模型，发现混淆引入延迟税，通用模型性能下降而推理模型保持准确率不变，为评估数学推理能力提供了量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在形式数学基准（如MiniF2F）上表现良好，但不清楚这些结果源于真正的逻辑推理还是对预训练数据的语义模式匹配。作者希望区分这两种能力，评估模型真正的数学推理能力。

Method: 通过重命名Lean 4中自然数游戏的标识符，创建零知识的封闭环境（混淆自然数游戏基准），评估包括通用模型（Claude-Sonnet-4.5, GPT-4o）和推理模型（DeepSeek-R1, GPT-5, DeepSeek-Prover-V2）在内的最先进模型。

Result: 混淆操作普遍增加了推理时间（延迟税）；通用模型准确率下降，而推理模型在缺少语义线索的情况下仍保持相同的准确率。

Conclusion: 架构推理是未来自动定理发现AI所需的关键能力；混淆自然数游戏可作为评估真正数学推理能力的定量指标；推理模型在架构推理上优于通用模型。

Abstract: While Large Language Models have achieved notable success on formal mathematics benchmarks such as MiniF2F, it remains unclear whether these results stem from genuine logical reasoning or semantic pattern matching against pre-training data. This paper identifies Architectural Reasoning: the ability to synthesize formal proofs using exclusively local axioms and definitions within an alien math domain, as the necessary ability for future automated theorem discovery AI. We use the Obfuscated Natural Number Game, a benchmark to evaluate Architectural Reasoning. By renaming identifiers in the Natural Number Game in Lean 4, we created a zero-knowledge, closed environment. We evaluate state-of-the-art models, finding a universal latency tax where obfuscation increases inference time. The results also reveal a divergence in robustness: while general models (Claude-Sonnet-4.5, GPT-4o) suffer performance degradation, reasoning models (DeepSeek-R1, GPT-5, DeepSeek-Prover-V2) maintain the same accuracy despite the absence of semantic cues. These findings provide a quantitative metric for assessing the true capacity for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [93] [Deep Kernel Learning for Stratifying Glaucoma Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00708)
*Bruce Rushing,Angela Danquah,Alireza Namazi,Arjun Dirghangi,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度核学习（DKL）架构的方法，利用高斯过程后端和Transformer特征提取器处理临床BERT嵌入，从稀疏不规则采样的多模态EHR中识别青光眼患者的高风险进展轨迹，成功区分出三个临床不同的亚组，其中高风险组虽当前视力较好但存在恶化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生需要从稀疏、不规则采样的电子健康记录中识别慢性疾病（如青光眼）的高进展风险患者，现有方法难以有效解耦疾病进展与当前严重程度。

Method: 提出DKL架构，采用高斯过程作为后端，通过基于Transformer的特征提取器处理临床BERT嵌入，对多模态EHR数据中的青光眼患者轨迹进行建模。

Result: 成功识别出三个临床不同的患者亚组；模型能够解耦疾病进展与当前严重程度，识别出一组虽然平均视力较好但轨迹恶化的高风险组。

Conclusion: 该模型能够基于风险轨迹进行患者分层，为临床决策支持提供有力工具，有助于对高风险个体实施针对性干预，改善青光眼护理管理。

Abstract: Effectively stratifying patient risk in chronic diseases like glaucoma is a major clinical challenge. Clinicians need tools to identify patients at high risk of progression from sparse and irregularly-sampled electronic health records (EHRs). We propose a novel deep kernel learning (DKL) architecture that leverages a Gaussian Process (GP) backend. The GP's kernel is defined by a transformer-based feature extractor applied to clinical-BERT embeddings to model glaucoma patient trajectories from multimodal EHR data. Our method successfully identifies three clinically distinct patient subgroups. Crucially, the model learns to decouple disease progression from current severity, identifying a high-risk group with a worsening trajectory despite having better average visual acuity than a second, stably poor group. This reveals that the model learns to identify progression risk rather than just the current disease state. This ability to stratify patients based on their risk trajectory progression offers a powerful tool for clinical decision support, enabling targeted interventions for high-risk individuals and improving the management of glaucoma care.

</details>


### [94] [Temporal Data Requirement for Predicting Unplanned Hospital Readmissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00738)
*Ramin Mohammadi,Vahab vahdat,Sarthak Jain,Amir T. Namin,Ramya Palacholla,Sagar Kamarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现在髋/膝关节置换术后30天再入院预测中，非结构化临床笔记的最佳历史数据窗口（3-6个月）显著短于结构化数据（12个月后趋于平稳），挑战了"更多历史数据带来更好预测"的普遍假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）数据激增，但构建预测模型时确定最优历史数据时间窗口是一个关键挑战，现有研究对此缺乏系统性探究。

Method: 使用来自7,174名患者的超过400万条结构化记录和80,000份临床笔记，采用多种非神经编码器（BOW、count BOW、TF-IDF、LDA）和神经编码器（BERT、1D CNN、BiLSTM、Average），分别评估仅使用临床笔记、仅使用结构化数据以及多模态组合下的预测性能。

Result: 临床笔记的最佳时间窗口为术前3-6个月，远短于结构化数据；结构化数据性能随窗口延长而提升但在12个月后趋于平稳；该模态特异性时间模式不受模型复杂度或编码器类型影响。

Conclusion: 挑战了"更多历史数据更好预测"的普遍假设，为优化再入院预测模型建立了针对性的时间窗口指南。

Abstract: With the proliferation of Electronic Health Records (EHRs), a critical challenge in building predictive models is determining the optimal historical data time window to maximize accuracy. This study investigates the impact of various observation windows ranging from the day of surgery to three years prior on predicting 30-day readmission following hip and knee arthroplasties. The dataset encompasses both structured encounter records (over 4 million) and unstructured clinical notes (80,000) from 7,174 patients. To extract meaning from the clinical notes, we employed a suite of non neural (BOW, count BOW, TF IDF, LDA) and neural encoders (BERT, 1D CNN, BiLSTM, Average). We subsequently evaluated models utilizing clinical notes alone, structured data alone, and a combination of both modalities. Our results demonstrate that the optimal time window for unstructured clinical notes is significantly shorter than for structured data, maximum predictive performance was achieved using notes from just three to six months prior to surgery. In contrast, performance using structured data improved as the time window lengthened, but strictly plateaued after twelve months. These modality-specific temporal patterns remained consistent regardless of model complexity or encoder type. Ultimately, these findings challenge the general assumption that more historical data inherently yields better machine learning predictions, establishing targeted time-window guidelines for optimizing readmission prediction models.

</details>


### [95] [SAVGO: Learning State-Action Value Geometry with Cosine Similarity for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00787)
*Stavros Orfanoudakis,Pedro P. Vergara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAVGO算法，通过联合状态-动作嵌入空间学习价值几何，利用相似性核引导策略更新，统一表征学习、价值估计和策略优化，在MuJoCo基准上表现优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征学习和相似性学习虽能提升强化学习样本效率，但很少被直接用于在动作空间中塑造策略更新，缺乏几何感知的策略优化方法。

Method: 学习联合状态-动作嵌入空间，使价值估计相似的动作对具有高余弦相似度；基于学习到的几何结构生成相似性核，引导策略向高价值区域更新，统一目标函数中融合表征学习、价值估计和策略优化。

Result: 在MuJoCo连续控制基准上，SAVGO在挑战性高维任务中优于强基线；消融实验验证了价值几何学习和基于相似性的策略更新的各自贡献。

Conclusion: SAVGO通过显式地将价值几何融入策略更新，超越了局部梯度更新的限制，实现了更全局的策略引导，同时保持离策略Actor-Critic的可扩展性。

Abstract: While representation and similarity learning have improved the sample efficiency of Reinforcement Learning (RL), they are rarely used to shape policy updates directly in the action space. To bridge this gap, a geometry-aware RL algorithm that explicitly incorporates value-based similarity into the policy update, State-Action Value Geometry Optimization (SAVGO), is proposed. In detail, SAVGO learns a joint state-action embedding space in which pairs with similar action-value estimates exhibit high cosine similarity, while dissimilar pairs are mapped to distinct directions. This learned geometry enables the generation of a similarity kernel over candidate actions sampled at each update, allowing policy improvement to be guided directly toward higher-value regions beyond local gradient-based updates. As a result, representation learning, value estimation, and policy optimization are unified within a single geometry-consistent objective, while preserving the scalability of off-policy actor-critic training. The proposed method is evaluated on standard MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks, demonstrating improvements over strong baselines on challenging high-dimensional tasks. Ablation studies are done to analyze the contributions of value-geometry learning and similarity-based policy updates.

</details>


### [96] [Generating Statistical Charts with Validation-Driven LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00800)
*Pavlin G. Poličar,Andraž Pevcin,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM的结构化图表生成流程，通过渲染验证、多步分解和全对齐数据产出，生成了包含1,500张图表和30,003个QA对的数据集，并评估了16个多模态大模型在图表推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM从表格数据生成图表时，存在渲染后可见但数据或代码层面无法检测的失败模式；同时缺乏代码、数据集、QA对完全对齐的图表数据集，难以支持细粒度的诊断研究。

Method: 将图表生成分解为数据集筛选、图表提案、代码合成、渲染、基于渲染验证的优化、描述生成和问答生成七个步骤，通过渲染输出验证解决可读性和语义不匹配等可视化特有的失败模式。

Result: 在74个UCI数据集上生成了1,500张图表（涵盖24种图表类型），附带30,003个QA对；对16个多模态大模型的评估表明图表语法类问题近乎饱和，而数值提取、比较和推理仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作流将图表生成从一次性提示转代码的任务转变为可检查的过程，并产出全对齐数据，为图表多模态推理的诊断研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Generating diverse, readable statistical charts from tabular data remains challenging for LLMs, as many failures become apparent after rendering and are not detectable from data or code alone. Existing chart datasets also rarely provide fully aligned artifacts, such as executable code, dataset context, and question-answer pairs. We present a structured LLM-based workflow that decomposes chart generation into dataset screening, plot proposal, code synthesis, rendering, validation-driven refinement, description generation, and question-answer generation. By incorporating rendered-output validation, the workflow addresses visualization-specific failure modes such as readability and semantic mismatch. It treats chart generation as an inspectable process rather than a one-shot prompt-to-code task, retaining each chart with its code, dataset context, description, and question-answer pairs. Applied to UCI datasets, the workflow produces 1,500 charts from 74 datasets, spanning 24 chart families and paired with 30,003 question-answer pairs. We evaluate 16 multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) on these chart-question pairs. The results show that chart-syntax questions are nearly saturated, while value extraction, comparison, and reasoning remain more challenging, illustrating the workflow's utility for diagnostic studies of chart-grounded multimodal reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Model Checking for Low Monodimensionality Fragments of CMSO on Topological-Minor-Free Graph Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00192)
*Ignasi Sau,Nicole Schirrmacher,Sebastian Siebertz,Giannos Stamoulis,Dimitrios M. Thilikos,Alexandre Vigny*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过引入"低单维度性"注释图参数，定义了CMSO的一个新片段，并证明在该片段中加入不相交路径谓词后，模型检测问题在拓扑子图封闭图类上是固定参数可解的，从而将多项已知算法元定理从一阶逻辑推广到拓扑子图禁止图类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的算法元定理中，一阶逻辑加不相交路径谓词（FO+dp）在排除固定拓扑子图类上具有高效模型检测，但更丰富的CMSO（计数单子二阶逻辑）片段在同类图类上的可处理性此前未知。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于Sau、Stamoulis和Thilikos [SODA 2025]提出的通过注释图参数划分CMSO的框架，将集合量化的范围限制在满足有界结构条件的顶点集上，具体定义为"低单维度性"（low monodimensionality），从而识别出一个新的CMSO片段。

Result: 该片段（加上不相交路径谓词）的模型检测问题在排除拓扑子图图类上是固定参数可解的（FPT），且这类图本质上界定了该逻辑在子图封闭类上的可处理性边界。

Conclusion: 该结果将多个已知算法元定理从FO提升到CMSO的丰富片段，拓展了拓扑子图禁止图类上的元定理边界。

Abstract: Algorithmic meta-theorems explain the tractability of large classes of computational problems by linking logical expressibility with structural graph properties. While extensions of first-order logic such as FO+dp admit efficient model checking on graph classes excluding a fixed topological minor, comparable results for richer fragments of CMSO were previously unknown. We further develop the framework of Sau, Stamoulis, and Thilikos [SODA 2025] for fragmenting CMSO via annotated graph parameters, which restrict set quantification to vertex sets satisfying bounded structural conditions. Following this approach, we identify a fragment of CMSO, namely the one defined by allowing quantification only over sets having what we call low monodimensionality, that generalizes several previously-known logics and we show that model checking for this fragment, enhanced with the disjoint-paths predicate, is fixed-parameter tractable on topological-minor-free graph classes. Such classes essentially delimit the tractability for this logic on subgraph-closed classes. As a consequence, our results lift several known algorithmic meta-theorems beyond first-order logic to the topological-minor-free setting.

</details>


### [98] [Polymorphism Meets DHOL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00295)
*Rhea Ranalter,Florian Rabe,Cezary Kaliszyk*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了多态依赖高阶逻辑（PDHOL），扩展了DHOL以支持多态，实现了更简洁的编码，并通过翻译至HOL获得自动化定理证明能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DHOL虽然通过依赖类型支持了有界数据结构、范畴论和证明论等领域的简洁编码，但缺乏多态性这一关键特性，限制了其表达能力和通用性。

Method: 开发了多态DHOL的语法和语义，并扩展了从DHOL到HOL的翻译方法；在逻辑嵌入工具中实现该翻译，并利用现成的HOL定理证明器构建PDHOL定理证明器。

Result: 在TPTP形式化基准上进行了评估，验证了翻译的有效性和自动化定理证明能力。

Conclusion: PDHOL通过引入多态性增强了DHOL的表达能力，结合HOL定理证明器可实现高效的自动化推理支持。

Abstract: DHOL is an extensional, classical logic that equips the well-known higher-order logic (HOL) with dependent types. This allows for concise encodings of important domains like size-bounded data structures, category theory, or proof theory. Automation support is obtained by translating DHOL to HOL, for which powerful modern automated theorem provers are available. However, a critically missing feature of DHOL is polymorphism. We develop the syntax and semantics of polymorphic DHOL and extend the translation accordingly. We implement the translation in the logic-embedding tool and evaluate it on a range of TPTP formalizations. The logic-embedding tool, together with an off-the-shelf HOL theorem prover easily creates a PDHOL theorem prover for experimenting.

</details>


### [99] [Efficient Incremental #SAT via Cross-Instance Knowledge Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00671)
*Uriya Bartal,Dror Fried,Jean-Marie Lagniez*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增量模型计数方法，通过持久化缓存机制和适配的分支启发式，在结构相似的公式序列中复用组件数据，避免冗余搜索，并在论证与软核问题中验证了其优于现有模型计数器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型计数（#SAT）是#P完全问题，在概率推理中至关重要。现有方法对每个公式独立求解，无法利用序列中结构相似公式间的计算重用机会，导致大量重复计算。

Method: 提出持久化缓存机制，在多次求解器调用间保留组件数据；并设计了适配增量场景的分支启发式策略。

Result: 在论证（argumentation）和软核（soft core）问题上，该方法相比现有模型计数器取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构感知的重用能力在动态环境中具有显著优势，增量模型计数能有效降低相似公式序列的计算开销。

Abstract: Model counting ($\#\text{SAT}$) is a fundamental yet $\#\text{P}$-complete problem central to probabilistic reasoning. In this work, we address \textit{incremental model counting}, where sequences of structurally similar formulas must be counted. We propose an approach that amortizes computation via a persistent caching mechanism, retaining component data across solver calls to avoid redundant search. Additionally, we investigate branching heuristics adapted for this setting. We focus on the problems of argumentation and soft core, for which incremental model counting is natural. Experiments demonstrate that our method improves performance compared to current model counters, highlighting the capability of structure-aware reuse in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [100] [Univalence without function extensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00812)
*Evan Cavallo,Jonas Höfer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究范畴单值性（categorical univalence）是否蕴含函数外延性（function extensionality），通过Von Glehn的多项式模型构造，证明范畴单值性并不蕴含函数外延性，即存在一个范畴单值的宇宙但函数外延性不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同伦类型论中，Voevodsky证明了函数外延性可以从单值性公理推出。本文试图探究一个更弱的变体——范畴单值性是否也具有相同的推论能力。

Method: 利用Von Glehn的多项式模型构造（polynomial model construction），分析当基础模型具有单值宇宙时，其多项式模型会产生一个范畴单值但缺乏函数外延性的宇宙。

Result: 范畴单值性不能推出函数外延性。具体地，存在一个模型，其宇宙是范畴单值的，但函数外延性在该模型中不成立。

Conclusion: 范畴单值性是比全单值性更弱的条件，它不足以推导出函数外延性，从而澄清了单值性公理与函数外延性之间的逻辑关系。

Abstract: It is a well-known theorem of homotopy type theory, originally due to Voevodsky, that function extensionality holds inside any univalent universe. We consider a weaker variant of the univalence axiom, asserting that the wild category formed by the universe is univalent, which we call categorical univalence. We show that categorical univalence does not imply function extensionality by an analysis of Von Glehn's polynomial model construction, which produces models of Martin-Löf type theory that always refute function extensionality. We find in particular that when the base model has a univalent universe, its polynomial model has a universe that is categorically univalent but lacks function extensionality.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Type Theory With Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00655)
*Constantine Theocharis,Edwin Brady*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二阶广义代数理论（SOGAT）的结构化抹除类型理论，将抹除编码为运行时项与抹除项之间的阶段区分，并通过范畴模型、保守性证明和预层模型验证了其语义正确性，最终在Agda中形式化并实现了玩具elaborator。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在类型理论中引入运行时相关与无关数据的区分，使得编译时能安全地抹除无关数据。现有系统（Agda、Idris、Rocq）已实现类似功能，但缺乏统一的结构化形式化框架。

Method: 将抹除编码为二阶广义代数理论（SOGAT），通过阶段区分（运行时 vs 抹除项）以命题形式出现在上下文中。利用带范畴的族（CwF）构建模型，研究集合族中的语义，并推广到任何配备微小命题的Grothendieck topos。通过预层模型构造非类型化λ演算程序，并使用粘合（gluing）证明正确性。

Result: 建立了抹除类型理论的范畴模型；证明了对Martin-Löf类型理论（MLTT）在两个阶段上的保守性；构造了用于代码提取的预层模型并通过粘合证明了其正确性；所有结果在Agda中形式化，并提供了玩具elaborator实现。

Conclusion: 抹除类型理论可以通过SOGAT统一且结构化地形式化，具有良好的模型论基础，兼容其他结构化特性（如阶段划分），并为实现提供了清晰指导。

Abstract: Erasure enriches type theory with a distinction between runtime relevant and irrelevant data, allowing the compilation step to safely erase the latter. Versions of this feature are implemented by many systems, including Agda, Idris, and Rocq. We present a structural version of type theory with erasure, formulated as a second-order generalised algebraic theory (SOGAT). Erasure is encoded as a phase distinction between runtime and erased terms, in the form of a proposition that can appear in a context. This formulation has several advantages: it has models based on categories with families, is compatible with other structural features such as staging, and provides a better guideline for implementation. Through the model theory of SOGATs, we study the semantics of type theory with erasure in families of sets, which generalises to any Grothendieck topos equipped with a tiny proposition. We establish conservativity over Martin-Löf type theory (MLTT) in both phases. For code extraction, we construct a presheaf model that produces untyped lambda calculus programs and prove its correctness through gluing. Our results are formalised in Agda and we provide a toy elaborator implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Symbolic Execution Meets Multi-LLM Orchestration: Detecting Memory Vulnerabilities in Incomplete Rust CVE Snippets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00034)
*Zeyad Abdelrazek,Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合符号执行（KLEE）与4智能体多LLM架构的系统，用于检测Rust不安全代码中的内存漏洞。系统通过四个专业化智能体协同生成KLEE兼容的测试框架，解决CVE不完整代码片段的编译失败问题。在31个真实Rust CVE上的评估表明，包装器编译成功率达90.3%（对比现有工具的0%），在26个文件中检测到1,206个关键错误（检出率83.9%），显著优于Clippy（35.5%）和Miri。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化验证工具因CVE数据库提供的代码片段不完整（缺少结构定义、导入和Cargo清单）而无法编译，导致所有工具输出为零。需要一种能够从不完整代码片段中合成可编译测试框架的方法，从而实现对Rust不安全代码中内存漏洞的自动化检测。

Method: 系统采用4智能体多LLM架构：Oracle/Validator负责战略规划、Safety Checker进行漏洞分析、Code Specialist生成FFI包装器、Fast Filter优化执行。四个智能体协作从不完整代码片段合成KLEE兼容的测试框架。KLEE输出由graph_klee.py处理，构建包含CVE文件、CWE类别、错误类型和符号执行路径的图数据库。

Result: 在31个涵盖11个CWE类别的真实Rust CVE上，包装器编译成功率达90.3%（对比现有工具的0%），检测到1,206个关键错误（26个文件，检出率83.9%），而Clippy仅检测到14个警告（11个文件，35.5%），Miri仅提供通用标签。4智能体架构相比单智能体基线将包装器编译失败率从42%降至9.7%，检测错误数从487增至1,206。

Conclusion: 角色专业化和结构化上下文传递比单个通用模型产生可衡量的更好结果。该系统成功将符号执行应用于此前无法分析的Rust不完整代码片段，显著提升了内存漏洞检测能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a system combining symbolic execution (KLEE) with a 4-agent multi-LLM architecture for detecting memory vulnerabilities in Rust unsafe code. A central challenge we address is the incomplete-code problem: CVE database entries provide only isolated code snippets that lack struct definitions, imports, and Cargo manifests, causing all existing formal verification tools to fail at compilation with zero output. Our system resolves this through four specialized agents -- an Oracle/Validator for strategic planning, a Safety Checker for vulnerability analysis, a Code Specialist for FFI wrapper generation, and a Fast Filter for execution optimization -- that collaboratively synthesize KLEE-compatible harnesses from otherwise uncompilable fragments. KLEE's output is then ingested by graph_klee.py, which constructs a Graph Database linking CVE files, CWE categories, error types, and symbolic execution paths as typed nodes and labelled edges, enabling structured cross-CVE vulnerability queries. We evaluated our system on 31 real-world Rust CVEs spanning 11 CWE categories, achieving 90.3% wrapper compilation success where all state-of-the-art formal verification tools achieve 0%. Our system detected 1,206 critical errors across 26 files (83.9% detection rate), compared to 14 warnings across 11 files for Clippy (35.5%) and generic labels for Miri. The 4-agent architecture reduced wrapper compilation failures from 42% (single-agent baseline) to 9.7% and increased detected errors from 487 to 1,206, confirming that role specialization and structured context passing produce measurably better results than a single general-purpose model. Our replication package is publicly available at https://github.com/Zeyad-Ab/Symbolic-Execution-with-Multi-LLM-Architecture-for-Rust-Security

</details>


### [103] [Defense against Poisoning Attacks under Shuffle-DP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00625)
*Siyi Wang,Qiyao Luo,Yihua Hu,Lixu Wang,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对shuffle-DP模型的通用防御框架，能够将任意保并集查询的shuffle-DP协议转化为抗投毒攻击的版本，在攻击存在时仅引入多对数级别的误差增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有shuffle-DP协议依赖于所有用户诚实的强假设，而现实场景中恶意用户可通过投毒攻击破坏隐私保证和效用；现有防御方案仅限于频率估计任务，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出一种通用防御框架，可将任意保并集查询的shuffle-DP协议转化为抗投毒攻击的版本。框架在无攻击时保持渐近等价误差，存在常数个攻击者时仅增加多对数级别的误差。

Result: 在求和、频率估计、范围计数等多种常见查询上验证了框架的通用性，实验结果表明该方法能有效防御投毒攻击，同时保持高效用和通信效率。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个针对shuffle-DP模型下投毒攻击的通用防御框架，填补了该领域除频率估计外其他查询类型的防御空白。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) has become the gold standard for protecting individual privacy in data analytics, and the shuffle-DP model has attracted significant attention from both academia and industry due to its favorable balance between privacy and utility. However, existing shuffle-DP protocols rely on a strong assumption: all users behave honestly. In real-world scenarios, adversarial users can exploit this vulnerability through poisoning attacks, compromising both privacy guarantees and the utility of analytical results. While defending against poisoning attacks in the shuffle-DP model has recently gained interest, existing solutions are limited to frequency estimation tasks. To address this issue, we propose the first general defense framework for all union-preserving queries, capable of transforming any shuffle-DP protocol into a version resilient to poisoning attacks. Beyond robust defense against poisoning attacks, our framework achieves high utility of analytical results. Compared to the original shuffle-DP protocol, it retains asymptotically equivalent error in attack-free settings and incurs only a polylogarithmic increase in error when a constant number of attackers are present. We demonstrate the generality of our framework on several common queries, including summation, frequency estimation, and range counting. Experimental results confirm that our approach effectively defends against poisoning attacks while maintaining strong utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [104] [Alignment Contracts for Agentic Security Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00081)
*Isaac David,Marco Guarnieri,Arthur Gervais*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"对齐契约"(alignment contracts)的形式化框架，用于在智能体安全系统中对可观测效应轨迹进行约束指定与强制执行，将安全边界作为头等正式契约，并给出了语义、验证、组合与可判定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全系统中的防护机制虽能提供策略控制，但未能将安全边界（系统在授权范围内保强攻击能力、同时在范围外禁止相同能力）作为可观测效应的头等正式契约，存在不对称控制问题。

Method: 定义了对齐契约框架，包含作用域、允许/禁止效应、资源预算和披露策略；给出有限迹语义，将满足性刻画为具有有限违反见证的安全性质；建立了精化规则和单向组合规则用于模块化契约工程；证明了可接受性检查的可判定性；在面向Web的智能体安全工作流上进行了实例化。

Result: 在显式效应可观测性假设下，证明了监控实现迹的强制执行可靠性；给出了假设提升的自适应结果；通过不可判定性转移和可观测性边界定理形式化了理论限制；在Lean 4中实现了核心定理的形式化验证。

Conclusion: 对齐契约为智能体安全系统提供了形式化的行为约束框架，使安全边界成为可验证的正式契约，理论上限被严格刻画，工具实现验证了核心定理的正确性。

Abstract: Agentic security systems increasingly combine LLM planners with tools that can discover, validate, and report vulnerabilities. This creates an asymmetric control problem: the system should retain strong offensive capability inside an authorized engagement, while the same capabilities must be denied outside scope. Existing guardrails provide useful policy controls, but they do not make this boundary a first-class formal contract over observable effects.
  We introduce alignment contracts, a framework for specifying and enforcing behavioral constraints over observable effect traces. A contract defines scope, allowed and forbidden effects, resource budgets, and disclosure policies. We give the language finite-trace semantics, characterize satisfaction as a safety property with finite violation witnesses, develop refinement and one-way composition rules for modular contract engineering, and show that admissibility checking is decidable. We instantiate the framework for web-focused agentic security workflows and show how the same structure extends to other effect profiles.
  Under an explicit Effect Observability Assumption, where all $\SigmaEff$-effects are mediated, the soundness theorem quantifies over the agent model and gives guarantees for mediated $\SigmaEff$-effects, including enforcement soundness for monitor-realized traces. We also state an assumption-lifted adaptation result and formalize limits through undecidability transfer and observability-boundary theorems. A Lean 4 artifact checks the formal core theorems used by the paper.

</details>


### [105] [zkSBOM: Privacy-Preserving SBOM Sharing with Zero-Knowledge Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00076)
*Tom Sorger,Eric Cornelissen,Aman Sharma,Javier Ron,Musard Balliu,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: zkSBOM是一种基于零知识集的隐私保护SBOM共享机制，允许消费者在不泄露额外SBOM内容的情况下查询漏洞，同时防止供应商篡改SBOM。安全分析和实际场景评估证明其有效性和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBOM共享机制在完全公开和完全隐藏之间只能二选一，导致软件供应商面临从SBOM推断出攻击的风险（如暴露存在漏洞依赖），而消费者则可能被篡改或虚假SBOM欺骗。

Method: 使用零知识集对SBOM中的组件进行密码学承诺；消费者可针对已知漏洞发起查询，接收密码学证明以确认SBOM描述的工件是否受影响，整个过程不泄露任何额外SBOM内容。

Result: 对zkSBOM进行了安全分析，量化了包含证明和排除证明的预期信息泄露；通过应用于真实场景并评估运行需求，证明了其实用可行性。

Conclusion: zkSBOM是一种强大、安全且隐私保护的SBOM共享机制，能够保护软件供应商和消费者免受彼此的攻击或欺骗。

Abstract: Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) are increasingly mandated by regulators, yet existing sharing mechanisms impose a binary choice between full disclosure and full opacity. This exposes software suppliers to attacks that can be deduced from the SBOM only, such as the presence of a vulnerable dependency. Conversely, software consumers can be fooled by software suppliers who modify or misrepresent published SBOMs. We present zkSBOM, a privacy-preserving SBOM sharing mechanism designed to address these threats. zkSBOM uses zero-knowledge sets to cryptographically commit to the components within an SBOM. Software consumers can query for known vulnerabilities and receive a cryptographic proof confirming whether the artifact described by the SBOM is affected, without revealing any additional SBOM content. We conduct a security analysis of zkSBOM by quantifying expected leakage from inclusion and exclusion proofs. We demonstrate real-world feasibility by applying it to realistic scenarios and evaluating its operation requirements. Our evaluation demonstrates that zkSBOM is a strong, secure, and privacy-preserving mechanism for SBOM sharing, protecting software suppliers and software consumers from one another.

</details>


### [106] [Zero-Knowledge Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00487)
*Pascal Berrang,Mirco Giacobbe,Jacob Swales,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合演绎模型检验与零知识证明的技术，能够在隐藏软件系统的前提下，公开验证其满足线性时序逻辑规范的正确性，并针对显式状态和符号化两种系统表示设计了具体方案，通过原型验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全攸关领域，软件系统的功能正确性与系统本身的机密性往往同等重要。现有形式化验证方法要求公开系统模型，无法在保护系统机密的同时进行正确性验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现不泄露系统本身即可完成形式化验证。

Method: 将演绎模型检验中的证明证书（如秩函数）与零知识证明协议相结合。显式状态方案使用多项式承诺来证明公开证书与秘密转移关系的一致性；符号化方案针对线性守卫命令系统，利用分段线性秩函数并应用Farkas引理构造公开/秘密分量的有效性见证，通过矩阵乘法的Sigma协议和范围证明来证明见证存在性。

Result: 构建了原型系统并在线性时序逻辑验证实例上展示了两种方案的实际可行性，证明了该方法能够在保持系统机密的前提下完成形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该技术使得在系统安全性和机密性都至关重要的领域（如专有软件、安全关键系统）中实现形式化验证成为可能，为隐私保护下的软件正确性证明提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We introduce a technology to formally verify that a software system satisfies a temporal specification of functional correctness, without revealing the system itself. Our method combines a deductive approach to model checking to obtain a formal certificate of correctness for the system, with zero-knowledge proofs to convince an external verifier that the system -- kept secret -- complies with its specification of correctness -- made public. We consider proof certificates represented as ranking functions, and introduce both an explicit-state and a symbolic scheme for model checking in zero knowledge. Our explicit-state scheme assumes systems represented as transition graphs. We use polynomial commitments to convince the verifier that the public proof certificates correspond to the secret transition relation. Our symbolic scheme assumes systems specified as linear guarded commands and uses piecewise-linear ranking functions. We apply Farkas' lemma to obtain a witness for the validity of the ranking function with public and secret components, and employ sigma protocols for matrix multiplication and range proofs to convince the verifier of the witness's existence. We built a prototype to demonstrate the practical efficacy of our two schemes on linear temporal logic verification examples. Our technology enables formal verification in domains where both the safety and the confidentiality of the system under analysis are critical.

</details>


### [107] [I can't recognize (yet): Delayed Rendering to Defeat Visual Phishing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00183)
*Ying Yuan,Cristiano Alex Rado,Giovanni Apruzzese,Mauro Conti,Luigi Vincenzo Mancini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示基于视觉的钓鱼网页检测器存在时间维度上的规避漏洞：攻击者可通过延时渲染关键元素（如Logo）来绕过检测，使检测率从100%降至0%，且用户难以察觉。作者提出了包括浏览器扩展在内的缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于视觉表示的机器学习方法在钓鱼网页检测中表现出色，但这类防御机制在设计上容易受到一种细微但低成本的基于时间的攻击。这种攻击此前从未被系统研究过，这种忽视质疑了该类防御在真实环境中的可靠性。

Method: 1) 揭示时间规避攻击的存在；2) 阐明视觉检测器的内在漏洞——通过延迟网页元素（如Logo）渲染，使截图在关键元素完全加载前完成，从而绕过视觉相似性检测；3) 使用窗帘效果等问题空间技术实证攻击效果；4) 进行用户研究评估攻击对真实用户的影响；5) 提出基于浏览器扩展的缓解方案。

Result: 状态最优的检测器检测率从100%降至0%；用户研究显示真实用户无法可靠识别扰动（p<.05）。

Conclusion: 基于视觉的钓鱼网页检测器存在严重的安全隐患，需要引入时间维度的防御机制。作者提出的浏览器扩展无需调用远程服务即可向用户发出警告。

Abstract: Phishing webpages are continuously polluting the Web. Plenty of countermeasures have been proposed and the most advanced techniques leverage machine-learning methods that infer whether a webpage is benign or not by inspecting its visual representation. Yet, despite the demonstrated effectiveness of such detection methods, this class of defenses is, by design, susceptible to a kind of subtle-but-cheap timing-based attacks which -- worryingly, and perhaps surprisingly -- have never been investigated so far. Such an oversight questions the overall reliability of these defenses in the wild.
  First, we show that timing-based evasion attacks have not been accounted for by prior work on visual phishing websites detectors. Then, we elucidate the intrinsic vulnerability of these detectors: they can be bypassed by delaying the rendering of webpage elements. Practically, these detectors must compute the visual similarity between a target webpage and a known legitimate one. This requires taking a "snapshot" of the target webpage before the similarity computation. Attackers can deliberately delay the rendering of key elements, such as the logo, so that these elements appear fully only after the snapshot has been taken. This simple tactic misleads the visual-similarity module, leading the system to incorrectly classify the phishing page as benign. We empirically show that state-of-the-art detectors can be completely defeated (detection rate dropping from 100% to 0%) by employing easy-to-apply problem-space techniques such as curtain effects. We also carry out a user study, evaluating the effectiveness of these attacks against real humans, and find that end users are unable to reliably identify our "perturbations" (p<.05). Finally, we propose mitigations, including a browser-extension that, without making any call to remote services, warns users that they may have landed on a phishing webpage.

</details>


### [108] [Skills as Verifiable Artifacts: A Trust Schema and a Biconditional Correctness Criterion for Human-in-the-Loop Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00424)
*Alfredo Metere*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了Agent技能应被视为不可信代码，运行时必须默认进行验证而非信任签名或来源；提出了包含验证等级、人工闸门策略、双条件正确性准则和十条规范指南的可移植运行时框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能作为一等部署工件后，运行时面临与包管理器相同的问题：内容声称行为，但运行时必须判断是否可信。现有信任机制（签名、权限、来源注册）不足以应对，而纯人工审核在规模上不可持续，会退化为橡皮图章式批准。

Method: 提出一个信任模式（包含技能清单上的显式验证等级）、能力闸门（人工审核策略为验证等级的函数）、双条件正确性准则（对抗性集成评估中验证程序必须满足的条件），以及从开源参考实现中抽象出的十条规范指南。方法对模型和框架无关，无需重新训练或微调。

Result: 通过对抗性集成评估验证了双条件正确性准则的有效性；实现了可移植的运行时配置文件；给出了一个可运行的开源参考实现。

Conclusion: 技能是不受信代码，运行时必须默认执行验证而非信任；将技能验证作为独立的有门控过程，人工审核只介入未验证部分，系统才可持续；所提框架与具体模型和基础设施解耦。

Abstract: Agent skills -- structured packages of instructions, scripts, and references that augment a large language model (LLM) without modifying the model itself -- have moved from convenience to first-class deployment artifact. The runtime that loads them inherits the same problem package managers and operating systems have always faced: a piece of content claims a behavior; the runtime must decide whether to believe it. We argue this paper's central thesis up front: a skill is \emph{untrusted code} until it is verified, and the runtime that loads it must enforce that default rather than infer trust from a signature, a clearance, or a registry of origin. Without skill verification, a human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate must fire on every irreversible call -- which is operationally untenable and degrades into rubber-stamping at any non-trivial scale. With skill verification treated as a separate, gated process, HITL fires only for what is unverified, and the system becomes sustainable. We give a trust schema (§\ref{sec:schema}) that includes an explicit verification level on every skill manifest; a capability gate (§\ref{sec:gate}) whose HITL policy is a function of that verification level; a \emph{biconditional} correctness criterion (§\ref{sec:biconditional}) that any candidate verification procedure must satisfy on an adversarial-ensemble exercise (§\ref{sec:eval}); and a portable runtime profile (§\ref{sec:guidelines}) with ten normative guidelines abstracted from a working open-source reference implementation \cite{metere2026enclawed}. The contribution is harness- and model-agnostic; nothing here requires retraining, fine-tuning, or proprietary infrastructure.

</details>


### [109] [Attention Is Where You Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00236)
*Aviral Srivastava,Sourav Panda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力重分布攻击（ARA），通过识别安全关键注意力头并生成非语义对抗性token，重定向安全相关位置的注意力，仅需5个token和500步优化即可绕过LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct和Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.1的安全对齐。机制研究发现安全行为源于注意力路由而非注意力头本身——消除头部可被残差流补偿，但重定向其注意力会传播损坏信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的大语言模型通过RLHF和指令微调拒绝有害请求，但其内部安全行为的实现机制尚不明确。现有越狱方法多在语义或输出logit层面操作，未能深入到注意力几何层面探索安全机制。

Method: 采用白盒对抗攻击方法，识别安全关键注意力头，利用Gumbel-softmax优化在概率单纯形上重定向softmax注意力分布，生成非语义对抗性token。通过消融实验与重分布实验的对比，揭示安全行为源于注意力路由机制而非头部本身。

Result: Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.1上ASR达到36%，LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct上30%，Gemma-2-9B-it仅1%。机制发现：零化安全头部最多仅导致39-50个基线拒绝中1个翻转，而重分布攻击使Mistral-7B上72/200、LLaMA-3上60/200提示翻转。

Conclusion: 安全行为不由特定注意力头作为可移除组件实现，而是源自这些头部执行的注意力路由——消除头部可通过残差流补偿，而重定向其注意力会向下游传播损坏信号。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models rely on RLHF and instruction tuning to refuse harmful requests, yet the internal mechanisms implementing safety behavior remain poorly understood. We introduce the Attention Redistribution Attack (ARA), a white-box adversarial attack that identifies safety-critical attention heads and crafts nonsemantic adversarial tokens that redirect attention away from safety-relevant positions. Unlike prior jailbreak methods operating at the semantic or output-logit level, ARA targets the geometry of softmax attention on the probability simplex using Gumbel-softmax optimization over targeted heads. Across LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.1, and Gemma-2-9B-it, ARA bypasses safety alignment with as few as 5 tokens and 500 optimization steps, achieving 36% ASR on Mistral-7B and 30% on LLaMA-3 against 200 HarmBench prompts, while Gemma-2 remains at 1%. Our principal mechanistic finding is a dissociation between ablation and redistribution: zeroing out the top-ranked safety heads produces at most 1 flip among 39 to 50 baseline refusals, while ARA targeting the corresponding safety-heavy layers flips 72/200 prompts on Mistral-7B and 60/200 on LLaMA-3. This suggests that safety is not localized in these heads as removable components, but emerges from the attention routing they perform. Removing a head allows compensation through the residual stream, while redirecting its attention propagates a corrupted signal downstream.

</details>


### [110] [A Privacy-Preserving Approach to Conformance Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00283)
*Luis Rodríguez-Flores,Luciano García-Bañuelos,Abel Armas-Cervantes,Astrid Rivera-Partida*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于同态加密和字符串处理算法的安全合规性检查方法，使得流程模型和事件日志在双方互不可见的情况下仍能进行对齐计算，尽管计算开销较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合规性检查需要分析者同时访问流程模型和事件日志，但在某些场景下，模型或日志包含敏感或关键信息，需要保护隐私。

Method: 采用基于对齐的合规性检查形式化框架，结合同态加密技术和字符串处理算法，实现在模型和日志双方互不可见条件下的安全计算。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实事件日志上的实验表明，该方法能够安全地完成合规性检查，但代价是高昂的内存和处理时间开销。

Conclusion: 同态加密可用于实现隐私保护的流程合规性检查，但当前性能开销较大，仍需优化以提升实用性。

Abstract: Conformance checking, one of the main process mining operations, aims to identify discrepancies between a process model and an event log. The model represents the expected behaviour, whereas the event log represents the actual process behaviour as captured in information systems records. Traditionally, the process model and the event log are both accessible to the business analyst performing the conformance checking. However, in some contexts, it is necessary to keep either the model or the log private to protect critical or sensitive information. In this paper, we propose a secure approach to conformance checking based on string processing algorithms and homomorphic encryption, where the process model and event log ar not visible to either the model's or event log's owner. The proposed technique is based on alignments, a well-known formalism used for conformance checking. An evaluation is performed using a synthetic and a real-world event log, showing that conformance checking can be securely computed at the expense of high memory and processing requirements.

</details>


### [111] [Trident: Improving Malware Detection with LLMs and Behavioral Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00297)
*Rebecca Saul,Jingzhi Jiang,Elliott Chia,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）处理沙箱行为报告，生成基于行为的恶意软件检测规则；并构建了Trident系统，融合静态特征决策树、行为规则和LLM投票分析，在保持低误报率的同时显著提升对概念漂移的鲁棒性，无需重训练即可达到与主动学习方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PE恶意软件检测依赖静态特征（如字节直方图、字符串信息、PE头内容），而动态分析特征因沙箱行为报告的半结构化特性难以有效利用。作者旨在利用新一代LLM的推理能力高效处理行为报告，将其纳入检测流程，以克服静态特征对概念漂移敏感的问题。

Method: 首先，利用LLM基于少量标注恶意软件样本生成行为驱动的检测规则；然后，构建Trident系统，集成三个模块：（1）基于静态特征的经典决策树模型，（2）基于LLM生成的行为检测规则，（3）直接对沙箱报告进行LLM多数投票分析。

Result: 行为检测规则相比静态特征方法对概念漂移具有更强的鲁棒性，同时保持实际可用的低误报率。Trident系统在性能上优于纯静态特征方法和纯行为规则方法，并且在无需重训练的情况下达到与主动学习方法同等的概念漂移鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效处理半结构化的沙箱行为报告并生成鲁棒的检测规则；多模态融合的Trident系统综合了静态与动态分析的优势，在概念漂移场景下具有显著优越性，为恶意软件检测提供了一种无需频繁重训练的实用方案。

Abstract: Traditionally, machine learning methods for PE malware detection have relied on static features like byte histograms, string information, and PE header contents. One barrier to incorporating dynamic analysis features has been the semi-structured nature of sandbox behavior reports. We show that, using the latest generation of large language models with reasoning, it is possible to efficiently process these behavior reports and utilize them as part of a malware detection pipeline. Specifically, we leverage LLMs to generate behavior-based malware detection rules based on a small training set of labeled malware. We find that these detection rules, derived from behavioral features, are much more robust to concept drift than standard static-feature methods, while maintaining practical false positive rates. Finally, we introduce Trident, a system which combines a classic decision tree model over static features, our behavior-based detection rules, and direct LLM analysis of sandbox reports through majority voting. Trident outperforms standard methods using static features, outperforms behavior-based rules alone, and is as resilient to concept drift as active learning methods without requiring retraining.

</details>


### [112] [Block-wise Codeword Embedding for Reliable Multi-bit Text Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00348)
*Joeun Kim,HoEun Kim,Dongsup Jin,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 现有LLM多比特水印方法因解码-检测混淆导致高误报率（FPR），本文提出BREW框架，通过分块投票估计与窗口平移验证两阶段机制，将范式转向指定验证，在10%同义词替换下达到TPR 0.965、FPR 0.02，证明高FPR并非多比特水印固有缺陷，而是解码中心设计的结构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于纠错码（ECC）的多比特水印提取器存在灾难性的误报率（FPR），且施加拒绝阈值会使检测灵敏度（TPR）退化为随机猜测，说明高FPR是解码中心式设计的结构性缺陷而非多比特水印的固有权衡。

Method: 提出BREW（分块可靠嵌入水印）框架：两阶段机制包括（i）盲消息估计：通过独立分块投票进行消息估计；（ii）窗口平移验证：对局部编辑进行严格的有效载荷验证。该框架与模型无关且具有理论基础。

Result: 在10%同义词替换攻击下，BREW达到0.965的TPR（真阳性率）和0.02的FPR（假阳性率），显著优于现有ECC-based方法。

Conclusion: 高FPR问题不是多比特水印的固有权衡，而是先前解码中心设计的可解结构性缺陷；BREW为可靠的取证部署提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent multi-bit watermarking methods for large language models (LLMs) prioritize capacity over reliability, often conflating decoding with detection. Our analysis reveals that existing ECC-based extractors suffer from catastrophic false positive rates (FPR), and applying rejection thresholds merely collapses detection sensitivity (TPR) to random guessing. To resolve this structural limitation, we propose \textbf{BREW} (Block-wise Reliable Embedding for Watermarking), a framework shifting the paradigm to \emph{designated verification}. BREW employs a two-stage mechanism: (i) \textbf{blind message estimation} via independent block voting, followed by (ii) \textbf{window-shifting verification} that rigorously validates the payload against local edits. Experiments demonstrate that BREW achieves a TPR of 0.965 with an FPR of 0.02 under 10\% synonym substitution, demonstrating that the high-FPR issue is not an inherent trade-off of multi-bit watermarking, but a solvable structural flaw of prior decoding-centric designs. Our framework is model-agnostic and theoretically grounded, providing a scalable solution for reliable forensic deployment.

</details>


### [113] [CleanBase: Detecting Malicious Documents in RAG Knowledge Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00460)
*Weifei Jin,Xilong Wang,Wei Zou,Jinyuan Jia,Neil Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CleanBase通过构建知识库文档的语义相似度图并检测恶意文档形成的团结构，来抵御针对RAG系统的提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）系统易受提示注入攻击，攻击者将包含精心构造注入提示的恶意文档插入知识库，当用户发起攻击目标的查询时，RAG系统可能检索到这些恶意文档并被误导生成攻击者指定的答案，从而破坏RAG系统的完整性。

Method: CleanBase的核心思路是：针对同一攻击目标问题构造的恶意文档之间通常具有高度的语义相似性。基于此，CleanBase在知识库上构建相似度图，每个节点代表一个文档，若两个文档的语义相似度（通过嵌入模型计算）超过统计确定的阈值则在它们之间连接边。由于内在相似性，恶意文档倾向于在该图中形成团结构。CleanBase检测这些团并将对应文档标记为恶意。

Result: 理论上推导了CleanBase的假阳性和假阴性率的上界。在多个数据集和提示注入攻击上的实验结果表明，CleanBase能够准确检测恶意文档并有效保护RAG系统。

Conclusion: CleanBase是一种有效的防御方法，通过检测知识库中语义相似的恶意文档团来抵御针对RAG系统的提示注入攻击。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, in which an adversary inserts malicious documents containing carefully crafted injected prompts into the knowledge database. When a user issues a question targeted by the attack, the RAG system may retrieve these malicious documents, whose injected prompts mislead it into generating attacker-specified answers, thereby compromising the integrity of the RAG system. In this work, we propose CleanBase, a method to detect malicious documents within a knowledge database. Our key insight is that malicious documents crafted for the same attack-targeted questions often exhibit high semantic similarity, as attackers deliberately make them consistent to improve attack success rates. Accordingly, CleanBase constructs a similarity graph over the knowledge database, where each node represents a document and an edge connects two nodes if their semantic similarity--computed using an embedding model--exceeds a statistically determined threshold. Due to their inherent similarity, malicious documents tend to form cliques within this graph. CleanBase detects such cliques and flags the corresponding documents as malicious. We theoretically derive upper bounds on CleanBase's false positive and false negative rates and empirically validate its effectiveness. Experimental results across multiple datasets and prompt injection attacks demonstrate that CleanBase accurately detects malicious documents and effectively safeguards RAG systems. Our source code is available at https://github.com/WeifeiJin/CleanBase.

</details>


### [114] [Pick and Sort for Graphical Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00558)
*Argianto Rahartomo,AmirHossein Jamshidipoor,Mohammad Ghafari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于“选择与排序”的图形化认证方案，用户通过选择视觉元素并在网格中排列进行身份验证。该方案支持可配置的元素数量和网格大小，并能针对特定用户群体（如儿童）定制视觉元素。初步原型研究表明该方案易学且部署灵活，尽管登录时间比传统方法长，但在非时间敏感场景（如低频访问或辅助认证）中可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认证方法（如密码）存在记忆负担和可用性问题，尤其在特定用户群体（如儿童）中更为突出。作者旨在提出一种更直观、易学且灵活的图形化认证方案，以替代或补充传统认证方式。

Method: 采用“Pick and Sort”设计范式：用户从一组视觉元素中选择若干元素，并将其排列在网格中指定位置。元素数量和网格大小均可配置，视觉元素可针对不同用户群体进行定制。通过原型实现开展了初步用户研究。

Result: 初步研究表明该方案易于学习和部署，但登录时间较传统认证方法更长。

Conclusion: 该图形化认证方案在非时间关键的场景（如低频访问或作为辅助认证机制）中具有实际应用价值，其易学性和灵活性弥补了登录时间较长的不足。

Abstract: We propose a graphical authentication scheme that follows a simple ``Pick and Sort'' design in which users choose visual elements and arrange them within a grid. Both the number of selected elements and the grid size are configurable, and the visual elements can be customized for specific user groups, such as children. A preliminary study with a prototype implementation indicated that the scheme is easy to learn and flexible to deploy. Although login times are longer than those of conventional authentication methods, the additional interaction may be acceptable in scenarios that are not time-critical, such as infrequent-access use cases or as a secondary authentication mechanism.

</details>


### [115] [KingsGuard: Enclave Data Protection Under Real-World TEE Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00613)
*Saltanat Firdous Allaqband,Deepanjali S,Rohit Srinivas R G,Devashish Gosain,Chester Rebeiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KINGSGUARD 是一种新型 TEE 设计，通过硬件支持的细粒度数据流追踪和检查，确保敏感数据不离开飞地边界，并支持受控的去分类机制，在 RISC-V 处理器上实现了 10.8% 的硬件面积开销和 5.69% 的性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TEE 的安全保证被飞地代码和底层硬件设计中的漏洞所破坏，敏感数据仍可能泄露，因此需要弥合理想化威胁模型与实际实现之间的差距。

Method: 提出 KINGSGUARD，一种在硬件层面实施细粒度数据流追踪和检查的 TEE 设计，系统性地监控和控制飞地内敏感数据的传播，并在飞地边界引入受控去分类机制。

Result: 在 RISC-V 处理器上实现，FPGA 综合后硬件面积开销为 10.8%，性能开销为 5.69%。

Conclusion: KINGSGUARD 通过硬件数据流追踪有效增强了 TEE 的安全性，能够在合理开销下防止敏感数据泄露，同时通过受控去分类保障了实际可用性。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have emerged as a cornerstone for securing sensitive computations by providing isolated enclaves protected from untrusted software. However, their security guarantees are undermined by vulnerabilities in both the enclave code and the underlying hardware design, which can allow sensitive data to leak despite strong isolation guarantees. This paper presents KINGSGUARD, a novel TEE design that systematically monitors and controls the propagation of sensitive data within an enclave. By enforcing fine-grained data flow tracking and checks in hardware, our approach ensures that sensitive data does not leave the enclave boundary, thus bridging the gap between the idealized threat models of TEEs and their practical realizations. Additionally, to balance security with practical functionality, we introduce controlled declassification at enclave boundaries, allowing intentional release of data to the outside world. Our implementation of KINGSGUARD on a RISC-V processor has a 10.8% hardware area overhead when synthesized on FPGA and a 5.69% performance overhead.

</details>


### [116] [STARE: Step-wise Temporal Alignment and Red-teaming Engine for Multi-modal Toxicity Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00699)
*Xutao Mao,Liangjie Zhao,Tao Liu,Xiang Zheng,Hongying Zan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: STARE提出分层强化学习框架，将扩散去噪轨迹作为攻击面，在白盒T2I+黑盒VLM设定下实现68%的攻击成功率提升，并发现了优化诱导的相位对齐现象——毒性语义在去噪不同阶段呈现结构化分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队攻击方法将图像生成视为黑盒，仅返回最终毒性分数，无法理解毒性语义在多步合成过程中何时以及如何涌现。

Method: 提出STARE分层强化学习框架：高层提示编辑器+低层T2I微调（GRPO），在白盒T2I和查询性黑盒VLM设定下，将去噪轨迹本身作为攻击面。

Result: 相比最先进的黑盒和白盒基线，攻击成功率提升68%。发现优化诱导的相位对齐现象：原始模型毒性分散，对抗优化将概念性危害集中到早期语义阶段、细节性危害集中到后期精炼阶段。

Conclusion: 毒性形成从混沌过程转变为可预测的脆弱窗口集合，为精准攻击引擎和相位感知安全机制提供基础。
